# General {#general}

The Developer Portal Documentation is a centralized platform designed to provide the developers with access to a comprehensive suite of resources, including API workflows, JSON samples for quick understanding and worked examples to kickstart the integration in an effective manner. This portal aims to foster collaboration by offering the necessary information essential for modernizing and streamlining the integration of Verteil Direct Connect (VDC) API’s.

# 

# Login {#login}

This section explains how to authenticate with the Verteil API:

**Login API**

User has to login in with the provided Username and password into the below URL:  
   
[https://api.stage.verteil.com/oauth2/token?grant\_type=client\_credentials\&scope=api](https://api.stage.verteil.com/oauth2/token?grant_type=client_credentials&scope=api)

| Authorization |  |
| :---- | :---- |
| **Type** | Basic Auth |
| **Username** |  |
| **Password** |  |

The execution of the Authorization gives an "access token". To call the services API across transactions, this “access token” should be supplied for the Authorization Type: "Bearer Token".

The token expiry time will be provided in seconds. The user has to ensure that the expiry time in the user's UI portal is set to less than the expiry time provided by Verteil.

All the relevant values in the HTTPS header of the login RQ including the key “User-Agent” has to be provided.

**Service API**

User can Access NDC Offer Management and NDC Order Management services using the below URL:

[https://api.stage.verteil.com/entrygate/rest/request](https://api.stage.verteil.com/entrygate/rest/request):\<APIName\>

The required Header details as well have to be provided by the user in the Request header.

| HTTPS Header |  |  |
| :---- | :---- | :---- |
| **Key** | **Value** | **Remarks** |
| Method | Post |  |
| Content-Type | application/json  |  |
| User-Agent | \<ApplicationName\>/\<version\>  | This is to be provided by the tech team of the API user based on the tech team company’s IT infrastructure  |
| Accept-Encoding | gzip | Value should be “gzip”  |
| ThirdpartyId | \<Owner Identifier\>  | This optional field allows the user to limit the search to offers from a specific airline. For example: LHG, BA, EK, SQ etc.If this field is left blank, the system will return offers from all airlines configured for the IATA..Refer to the sections [All Airline Search](#heading=h.fnd49vmaig0h) and [Airline Specific Search](#heading=h.bb8p5cwjvz8x) for more details. |
| OfficeId | \<internal code related to the IATA\> | Corresponding to the IATA configured in Verteil Verteil will provide the value to the tech team to supply in the HTTPS header of every API service  |
| Service | \<API service\>  | AirShopping, OrderCreate, SeatAvailability, etc.,  |
| Resource | \<value\> | This key is required only for the following API flows: Void / cancel ticketed booking, Cancel an unpaid booking Deferred payment flow Reissue ticket flow |

# 

# 

# Messages {#messages}

**AirShopping** : The **VDC AirShopping API** allows users to search for available flights based on specific travel criteria, such as origin, destination, travel dates, number of passengers, Passenger Type, Fare Type, Account code/Deal code, Frequent Flyer numbers and cabin class.

**FlightPrice** : The **VDC FlightPrice API** allows users to request a price breakdown and detailed fare conditions for a specific flight offer retrieved from a prior **AirShopping** response. This API ensures that the price is valid before proceeding with the booking.

**ServiceList** : The **VDC ServiceList API** allows users to request a list of ancillary services (such as baggage, seat selection, in-flight meals, lounge access, and priority boarding) available for a specific flight booking or offer in prime and post booking.

**SeatAvailability** : The **VDC SeatAvailability API** is used to request real-time seat availability for flight segments. It allows travelers to view available seats, their characteristics (e.g., extra legroom, window/aisle, premium seating), and any applicable pricing before selecting a seat during booking or post-booking.

**OrderCreate** : The **VDC OrderCreate API** is used to create an airline booking (Order) based on an accepted flight offer retrieved from a previous **VDC FlightPrice** response. This API can be used to create both instant booking and held booking.

**OrderRetrieve** : The **VDC OrderRetrieve API** allows users to retrieve details of an existing airline booking (Order) using an **Order ID**.

**OrderReshop (Reprice.V3)** : The **VDC OrderReshop** **API** allows users to request a reprice of the unpaid order before confirming changes

**OrderReshop (Reshop.V3)** : The **VDC OrderReshop** **API** allows users to request a modification of an existing booking (Order). Users can request for date change, cabin upgrade etc.

**OrderReshop(Requote.V3) :** The **VDC OrderReshop API** allows users to request a price breakdown and detailed fare conditions for a specific offer retrieved from a prior OrderReshop (Reshop.V3) response. This API ensures that the price is valid before proceeding with the confirmation.

**OrderChange** : The **VDC OrderChange** is used to request modifications to an existing airline order by addition of ancillaries, seats, APIS details etc..

**OrderChange(AcceptRepricedOrder.V3):** The **VDC** **OrderChange(AcceptRepricedOrder.V3)** allows users to inform the airline that the user is accepting the proposed Order, as it has been repriced and returned in the preceding OrderReshop(Reprice.V3) response.

**OrderChange(UpdateOrderItems.V3):** The **VDC OrderChange (UpdateOrderItems.V3)** allows the user to commit changes to an existing Order by confirming modification of itinerary such as date change, cabin upgrade etc.

**OrderChange(DivideOrderItems.V3):** The **VDC** **OrderChange (DivideOrderItems.V3) API** allows the user to request the airline to split the PNR by deleting passengers from the parent PNR (if the airline allows the same). This is preceded by OrderRetrieveRQ and the passenger reference IDs to be deleted are sent to this request along with passenger details.

**OrderChange(UpdatePassengers.V3):** The **VDC** **OrderChange (UpdatePassengers.V3)** allows users to request the airline to commit changes to an existing passenger. This change includes name change and the request includes the previous name (given/surname/titlename) and the new name (given/surname/title name) to which the name of the passenger should be changed.

**OrderChange (AcceptChange.V3):** The **VDC** **OrderChange (AcceptChange.V3)** allows users to accept a change as advised by the airline on an event of involuntary schedule change.

**ItinReshop** : The **VDC** **ItinReshop** is used prior to cancelling an order to retrieve information on the amounts paid for the original order, including a breakdown of taxes, applicable cancellation penalties, the refundable amount the user is eligible to receive from airline, and any amount owed by the passenger to the airline.

**OrderCancel** : The **VDC OrderCancel** is used to confirm and execute the cancellation of an order.

# **Workflows** {#workflows}

### 

### **Paid Booking** {#paid-booking}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate)

### **Retrieve Booking** {#retrieve-booking}

Retrieve an OrderID (OrderRetrieve)

### **Deferred ticketing** {#deferred-ticketing}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) →Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Reprice (OrderReshop(Reprice.V3)) → Payment (OrderChange(AcceptRepriced.V3))

### **Paid booking with ancillaries and seat**  {#paid-booking-with-ancillaries-and-seat}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Add Extras (ServiceList) → Add seats (SeatAvailability) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate with Ancillary and Seat)

### **Paid booking with ancillaries and seat which requires pricing** {#paid-booking-with-ancillaries-and-seat-which-requires-pricing}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Add Extras (ServiceList) → Add seats (SeatAvailability) → Price Seat and Ancillary (FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate with Ancillary and Seat)

### **Modify Itinerary (Reissue/ Revalidation)** {#modify-itinerary-(reissue/-revalidation)}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Reshop (OrderReshop(Reshop.V3)) → Price Offers (OrderReshop(Requote.V3)) → Payment (OrderChange(UpdateOrderItems.V3)) 

### **Post booking seat** {#post-booking-seat}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Add seats (SeatAvailability) → Payment (OrderChange)

### **Post booking seat which requires pricing** {#post-booking-seat-which-requires-pricing}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Add seats (SeatAvailability) → Price seat(FlightPrice) → Payment (OrderChange)

### **Post booking ancillary**  {#post-booking-ancillary}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Add Extras (ServiceList) → Payment (OrderChange)

### **Post booking ancillary which requires pricing** {#post-booking-ancillary-which-requires-pricing}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Add Extras (ServiceList) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Payment (OrderChange)

### **Cancellation for a paid booking** {#cancellation-for-a-paid-booking}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) → Book and Pay (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve)→ Refund quote (ItinRehop)→ Cancel booking (OrderCancel)

### **Cancellation for an unpaid booking** {#cancellation-for-an-unpaid-booking}

Shop for Flight (AirShopping) → Price offers(FlightPrice) →  Book (OrderCreate) → Retrieve (OrderRetrieve) → Reprice (OrderReshop(Reprice.V3)) → Cancel booking (OrderCancel)

## 

## 

## 

## 

## Airline Specific Search {#airline-specific-search}

Airline Specific Search is a system feature where users input their **origin** and **destination** for travel with a specific airline. If the user provides a **ThirdPartyID** in the header, the system will return flight offers exclusively from the airline associated with the given ThirdPartyID.This feature allows users to quickly access flight offers from a specific airline or airline group. For example, if a user wants to see only Emirates flights, the ThirdPartyID should be set to EK, and the system will return offers exclusively from Emirates. 

## All Airline Search {#all-airline-search}

The **All Airline Search** feature allows users to input their desired origin and destination for travel, returning a list of available flight offers across multiple airlines. This is particularly useful for travelers who are open to flying with any airline and want to explore all possible options. When **ThirdPartyID** is not provided, the system automatically returns flight offers from all airlines onboarded with the agency for the specified route.

## Search using shopping result preferences and sorting options {#search-using-shopping-result-preferences-and-sorting-options}

When a user searches a  flight, Verteil APIs provide the feature to manage the search results using the shop results preference such as Full, Optimised, or Best. These selections influence the nature of the results retrieved from the backend. After the results are fetched, they are sorted based on the chosen Sorting Criteria, such as Stop, Price, Departure, to display the most relevant options first. Please read more [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1uPSzhQw2tT244cMfKqCYiM52ZxB8Ga68msO3jW7VSU8/edit?usp=sharing).

# 

# Use cases  {#use-cases}

## Shop for One Way Flights {#shop-for-one-way-flights}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case}

This use case allows users to search for one-way flights based on origin, destination, departure date, and passenger details (e.g., DXB–LHR). It supports instant ticketing, enabling users to select a flight, add optional ancillaries and seats, and complete booking and ticket issuance in a single, seamless flow.

#### Process {#process}

**AirShopping**  
The user initiates a One way search for multiple PAX for flights via the VDC system. The VDC AirShoppingRQ structure will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "INF" //Infant            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "CHD" //Child            }            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "CoreQuery": {    "OriginDestinations": {      "OriginDestination": \[        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "LHR"            },            "Date": "2025-06-26"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "DXB"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD1" //Origin destination onward        }      \]    }  }} |
| :---- |

#### 

#### Success {#success}

A successful transaction means the user completes a **one-way flight booking** smoothly:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during VDC OrderCreateRQ). 

[Click here to view worked examples on one way shop to book](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1SKdaOF-Wq3VqWzKCsM1xm0fjydtPJ6yN?usp=drive_link)

## Shop for Round Trip Flights  {#shop-for-round-trip-flights}

## Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-1}

This use case allows users to search for round-trip flights (e.g., DXB–SIN–DXB) based on criteria like origin, destination, dates, and number of passengers. It supports instant ticketing, enabling users to select flights, add optional ancillaries (e.g., baggage, seats), and complete booking and ticket issuance in one seamless flow.

#### Process {#process-1}

**AirShopping**  
The user initiates a Round trip search for multiple PAX for flights via the VDC system. The VDC AirShoppingRQ structure will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "INF" //Infant            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "CHD" //Child            }           }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "CoreQuery": {    "OriginDestinations": {      "OriginDestination": \[        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "HEL"            },            "Date": "2025-06-18"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "FRA"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD1" //Origin destination onward        },        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "FRA"            },            "Date": "2025-06-25"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "HEL"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD2" //Origin destination return        }      \]    }  }} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID,Pax details and Origin Destination details for the offer that was selected. A sample snippet for VDC FlightPriceRQ for **Round trip** **MultiplePAX** would look like below:

#### 

#### Success {#success-1}

A successful transaction means that the user completes a **round-trip flight booking** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers for a round trip request  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during VDC OrderCreateRQ) in the response OrderViewRS

[Click here to view worked examples on round trip shop to book](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1oE1RhqsedeVyL64EkVC0xFLd_La9hWZV?usp=drive_link)

## 

## Shop for MultiCity Flights {#shop-for-multicity-flights}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-2}

The **Multicity Shopping** request allows users to search for flights with multiple stops by specifying criteria such as origin, destination, departure dates, and the number of users. Users can include up to **5 origin-destination pairs (ODs)** in a single request.The API will return a variety of available flight options, including detailed pricing, fare information for the journey.

#### Process {#process-2}

**AirShopping**  
The user initiates a Multicity search for multiple PAX for flights via the VDC system. The VDC AirShoppingRQ structure will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      }, {            "PTC": {              "value": "INF" //Infant            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "CHD" //Child            },           }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "CoreQuery": {    "OriginDestinations": {      "OriginDestination": \[        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "SYD"            },            "Date": "2025-06-04"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "MEL"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD1" //Origin destination 1        },        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": { "2025-06-09"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "PER"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD3" //Origin destination 3        },        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "PER"            },            "Date": "2025-06-11"          },          "Arrival": { **FlightPrice:**

A sample snippet for VDC FlightPriceRQ for **Multi city MultiplePAX** would look like below:

#### Success {#success-2}

A successful transaction means that the user completes a **MultiCity-trip flight booking** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers for a round trip request  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during VDCOrderCreateRQ)

[Click here to view worked examples on multicity trip shop to book](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1QUgGZ70gUSJVOM4MOXRTFJdUEqZF3-tI?usp=drive_link)

### 

## Shop for Open Jaw Flights {#shop-for-open-jaw-flights}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-3}

The Open-Jaw search allows a passenger to arrive in one city and depart from another, or return from a different city than the one originally flown to.  
*Example: Flying from New York to Paris, then returning from Rome to New York.*

#### Process {#process-3}

**AirShopping**  
The user initiates a Open jaw search for multiple PAX for flights via the VDC system. The VDC AirShoppingRQ structure will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "INF" //Infant            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "CHD" //Child            },          }        \]      }    \]  },  "CoreQuery": {    "OriginDestinations": {      "OriginDestination": \[        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "CDG"            },            "Date": "2025-05-23"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "DXB"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD1" //Origin Destination onward        },        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "BKK"            },            "Date": "2025-05-30"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "CDG"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD2" //Origin destination return        }      \]    }  }} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice:**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID,Pax details and Origin Destination details for the offer that was selected. A sample snippet for VDC FlightPriceRQ for **Open jaw search for MultiplePAX** would look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT", //Adult              "Quantity": 1            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT", //Adult              "Quantity": 1            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "INF", //Infant              "Quantity": 1            }          }        \]      },      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "CHD", //Child              "Quantity": 1            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "Query": {    "OriginDestination": \[ // Origin and destination (O\&D) details. This must be repeated for each O\&D.      {        "Flight": \[          {            "SegmentKey": "26-SEG12",            "Departure": {              "AirportCode": {                "value": "CDG"              },              "Date": "2025-05-23T00:00:00.000"            },            "Arrival": {              "AirportCode": {                "value": "DXB"              },              "Date": "2025-05-23T00:00:00.000"            },            "MarketingCarrier": {...},            "OperatingCarrier": {...},            "Equipment": {...},            "ClassOfService": {...}          }        \]      },      {        "Flight": \[...\]      }    \],    "Offers": {      "Offer": \[        {          "OfferID": { //OfferID from AirShoppingRS            "value": "c8ebd425-0a74-444f-abd4-250a74b400aa",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OfferItemIDs": {            "OfferItemID": \[ //OfferItemID from FlightPriceRS              {                "value": "ec20008f-f90a-4b29-a000-8ff90a2b290f",                "refs": \[                  "26-PAX1",                  "26-PAX2"                \]              },              {                "value": "b9b014e1-e032-4fad-b014-e1e032ffad8a",                "refs": \[                  "26-PAX3"                \]              },              {                "value": "e789372b-15ce-41e2-8937-2b15ce51e21d",                "refs": \[                  "26-PAX4"                \]              }            \]          }        }      \]    }  },  "DataLists": {...},  "ShoppingResponseID": {    "Owner": "26",    "ResponseID": { // Shopping response id from AirShoppingRS      "value": "CmwpDN6SqbWhf-IJsweGLF5CPlOueUctxDTrIMN8Qmw-26"    }  }} |

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId,pax details and DetailedFlightItem details. A sample Open jaw OrderCreateRQ for Multiple PAX would look like below:

#### 

#### Success {#success-3}

A successful transaction means that the user completes an **Open Jaw flight booking** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers for a round trip request  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate)

[Click here to view worked examples on openjaw trip shop to book](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1vH8Vfl-ceB6mfaNBMupyq8Np6GmKlHl4?usp=drive_link)

## 

## Shop for Offers with Check-in Baggage {#shop-for-offers-with-check-in-baggage}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-4}

This feature improves the flight search experience by letting users filter results to see only offers that include checked baggage. By removing fares without checked bags, it saves time and ensures a more relevant booking process for travelers who need this option. This way, passengers can focus only on the offers that match their needs.

#### Process {#process-4}

**AirShopping**

The user initiates a flight search and applies the check-in baggage filter. The AirShoppingRQ request is constructed to include the specific qualifier for this feature. The request will look like this:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT"            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "CoreQuery": {    "OriginDestinations": {      "OriginDestination": \[        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "LAX"            },            "Date": "2025-09-15"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "LHR"            }          }        }      \]    }  },  "Qualifier": {    "BaggagePricingQualifier": {      "BaggageOption": \[        {          "Code": "CheckedBag" //Filter for Checked bag        }      \]    }  }} |
| :---- |

## 

FlightPrice

Once the user selects a particular offer from the **AirShoppingRS** response, the **FlightPriceRQ** has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. A sample snippet for **FlightPriceRQ** would look like this:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT",              "Quantity": 1            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "Query": {    "OriginDestination": \[      {        "Flight": \[          {            "SegmentKey": "SEG1",            "Departure": {              "AirportCode": {                "value": "LAX"              },              "Date": "2025-09-15T00:00:00.000"            },            "Arrival": {              "AirportCode": {                "value": "LHR"              },              "Date": "2025-09-16T00:00:00.000"            },            "MarketingCarrier": {              ...            },            "OperatingCarrier": {              ...            },            "Equipment": {              ...            },            "ClassOfService": {              ...            }          }        \]      }    \],    "Offers": {      "Offer": \[        {          "OfferID": {            "value": "e789372b-15ce-41e2-8937-2b15ce51e21d",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OfferItemIDs": {            "OfferItemID": \[              {                "value": "ec20008f-f90a-4b29-a000-8ff90a2b290f",                "refs": \[                  "26-PAX1"                \]              }            \]          }        }      \]    }  },  "DataLists": {    ...  },  "ShoppingResponseID": {    "Owner": "26",    "ResponseID": {      "value": "CmwpDN6SqbWhf-IJsweGLF5CPlOueUctxDTrIMN8Qmw-26"    }  }} |
| :---- |

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId,pax details and DetailedFlightItem details. A sample OrderCreateRQ for Single Adult would look like below:

| {  "Query": {    "Passengers": {      "Passenger": \[        {          "ObjectKey": "PAX1",          "PTC": {            "value": "ADT"  // Adult          },          "Name": {            "Surname": {              "value": "DOE"            },            "Given": \[              {                "value": "JON"              }            \],            "Title": "Mr"          },          "AdditionalRoles": {            "PaymentContactInd": true          },          "Contacts": {            "Contact": \[              {......},                "PhoneContact": {                  "Application": "Home",                  "Number": \[                    {                      "value": "9987655232",                      "CountryCode": "91"                    }                  \]                }              }            \]          },          "Age": {            "BirthDate": {              "value": "1992-05-13"            }          },          "Gender": {            "value": "Male"          }        },      \]    },    "OrderItems": {      "ShoppingResponse": {        "Owner": "26",        "Offers": {.....},        "ResponseID": { //ResponseID from FlightPriceRS          "value": "CmwpDN6SqbWhf-IJsweGLF5CPlOueUctxDTrIMN8Qmw-26"        }      },      "OfferItem": \[        {          "OfferItemID": { //OfferItemID from FlightPriceRS            "value": "ec20008f-f90a-4b29-a000-8ff90a2b290f",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OfferItemType": {....}            \]          }        }      \]    },    "DataLists": {...}  }} |
| :---- |

#### Success {#success-4}

A successful transaction means that the user completes a flight search with the check-in baggage filter applied, and receives offers that meet the criteria.

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers that have Checked baggage.

✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare for the selected offer, ensuring transparency.

✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing immediate confirmation.

**Mapping Document**

| AirShoppingRS | FlightPriceRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/OfferID/value | Query/Offers/Offer/OfferID/value |
| OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/OfferID/Owner | Query/Offers/Offer/OfferID/Owner |
| OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/OfferID/Channel | Query/Offers/Offer/OfferID/Channel |

| OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/PricedOffer/OfferPrice/RequestedDate/Associations/AssociatedTraveler/TravelerReferences | Query/Offers/Offer/OfferItemIDs/OfferItemID/refs |
| DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/SegmentKey | Query/OriginDestination/Flight/SegmentKey |
| DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/AirportCode/value | Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Departure/AirportCode/value |
| DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Date | Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Departure/Date |
| DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Arrival/AirportCode/value | Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Arrival/AirportCode/value |
| DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList/AnonymousTraveler/ObjectKey | DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList/AnonymousTraveler/ObjectKey |
| DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList/AnonymousTraveler/PTC/value | DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList/AnonymousTraveler/PTC/value |
| Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/DescriptionMetadatas/DescriptionMetadata/AugmentationPoint/AugPoint/Key | ShoppingResponseID/ResponseID/valueNOTE: In case of an aggregated search the ShoppingResponseID value of the offer responsible airline should be used. |

## Shopping and booking with Seat and Ancillary which does not require pricing {#shopping-and-booking-with-seat-and-ancillary-which-does-not-require-pricing}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-5}

The user can shop for services—e.g., bags, meals, wheelchair assistance, etc.—using the **VDC ServiceList API**. Seat selection can be done through the **VDC SeatAvailability API.** Once the required services and seats are selected, if the **PricedInd is true**, it confirms that the selected ancillaries and seats have already been priced. In this case, there is no need to make an additional call to the FlightPrice API. The user can then proceed directly to complete the booking using the **VDC OrderCreate API.** To know more about PricedInd, please refer to the **Concept** section.

#### 

#### Process {#process-5}

**AirShopping**

The user searches for the flight. The ShoppingRQ for one way search for one PAX will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "CoreQuery": {    "OriginDestinations": {      "OriginDestination": \[        {          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "BOM"            },            "Date": "2025-03-29"          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "SIN"            }          },          "OriginDestinationKey": "OD1" //Origin destination onward        }      \]    }  }} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. The FlightPriceRQ for one way search for one PAX will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT", //Adult              "Quantity": 1            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "Query": {    "OriginDestination": \[      {        "Flight": \[...\]      }    \],    "Offers": {      "Offer": \[        {          "OfferID": {  //OfferId from AirShoppingRS            "value": "1H126Z\_37VVHBZJE4EMX3UZ6S86FGMQVOBY",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OfferItemIDs": {            "OfferItemID": \[ //OfferItemID from AirShoppingRS              {                "value": "1H126Z\_37VVHBZJE4EMX3UZ6S86FGMQVOBY-1-1",                "refs": \[                  "26-PAX1"                \]              }            \]          }        }      \]    }  },  "DataLists": {...},  "ShoppingResponseID": {    "Owner": "26",    "ResponseID": {      "value": "iqAIwcY2gr0h8T5oz7adzDx2Gf6sV6XdNuxZe3QSr4M-SQ"    }  }} |
| :---- |

**ServiceList:**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC FlightPriceRS, the VDC ServiceListRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. The ServiceListRQ for one way for one PAX search will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "ObjectKey": "PAX1",            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT" //Adult            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "Query": {    "OriginDestination": \[      {        "Flight": \[          {            "SegmentKey": "SEG2",            "Departure": {              "AirportCode": {                "value": "BOM"              },              "Date": "2025-03-29T11:50:00.000",              "Time": "11:50",              "AirportName": "Chhatrapati Shivaji International Airport",              "Terminal": {                "Name": "2"              }            },            "Arrival": {              "AirportCode": {                "value": "SIN"              },              "Date": "2025-03-29T19:50:00.000",              "Time": "19:50",              "AirportName": "Changi Airport",              "Terminal": {                "Name": "0"              }            },            "MarketingCarrier": {...},            "Equipment": {...},            "FlightDetail": {...}          }        \]      }    \],    "Offers": {      "Offer": \[        {          "OfferID": {            "value": "1H126Z\_37VVHBZJE4EMX3UZ6S86FGMQVOBY",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OfferItemIDs": {            "OfferItemID": \[              {                "value": "1H126Z\_37VVHBZJE4EMX3UZ6S86FGMQVOBY-1-1"              }            \]          }        }      \]    }  },  "ShoppingResponseID": {    "ResponseID": {      "value": "iqAIwcY2gr0h8T5oz7adzDx2Gf6sV6XdNuxZe3QSr4M-SQ"    }  }} |

**SeatAvailability:**

The VDC **SeatAvailability API** allows users to search for seats per passenger based on the availability from the airline. The API has the ability to retrieve seat maps for one or more flight segments. This service returns a priced seat map for the requested flight segments in a requested cabin.

The SeatAvailabilityRQ of one way one PAX will look like below:

| {  "Travelers": {    "Traveler": \[      {        "RecognizedTraveler": \[...\],        "AnonymousTraveler": \[          {            "ObjectKey": "PAX1",            "PTC": {              "value": "ADT"            }          }        \]      }    \]  },  "Query": {    "OriginDestination": \[      {        "FlightSegmentReference": \[          {            "ref": "SEG2"          }        \]      }    \],    "Offers": {      "Offer": \[        {          "OfferID": {            "value": "1H126Z\_37VVHBZJE4EMX3UZ6S86FGMQVOBY",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OfferItemIDs": {            "OfferItemID": \[              {                "value": "1H126Z\_37VVHBZJE4EMX3UZ6S86FGMQVOBY-1-1"              }            \]          }        }      \]    }  },  "DataLists": {    "FlightSegmentList": {      "FlightSegment": \[        {          "SegmentKey": "SEG2",          "Departure": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "BOM"            },            "Date": "2025-03-29T11:50:00.000",            "Time": "11:50",            "AirportName": "Chhatrapati Shivaji International Airport",            "Terminal": {              "Name": "2"            }          },          "Arrival": {            "AirportCode": {              "value": "SIN"            },            "Date": "2025-03-29T19:50:00.000",            "Time": "19:50",            "AirportName": "Changi Airport",            "Terminal": {              "Name": "0"            }          },          "MarketingCarrier": {...},          "Equipment": {...},          "FlightDetail": {...}        }      \]    },    "FareList": {      "FareGroup": \[        {          "ListKey": "FG-120813743178554",          "Fare": {            "FareCode": {              "Code": "749"            }          },          "FareBasisCode": {            "Code": "W14IIOB1"          } **OrderCreate:**

| Services/Service/Associations/Traveler/TravelerReferences | Query/OrderItems/OfferItem/OfferItemType/OtherItem/refs |
| Services/Service/ObjectKey | Query/OrderItems/OfferItem/OfferItemType/OtherItem/refs |
| Services/Service/Price | Query/OrderItems/OfferItem/OfferItemType/OtherItem/Price |
| Services/Service/Price/Total | Query/OrderItems/OfferItem/OfferItemType/OtherItem/Price/SimpleCurrencyPrice |
| Services/Service/Price/Total/value | Query/OrderItems/OfferItem/OfferItemType/OtherItem/Price/SimpleCurrencyPrice/value |
| Services/Service/Price/Total/Code | Query/OrderItems/OfferItem/OfferItemType/OtherItem/Price/SimpleCurrencyPrice/Code |

#### Success {#success-5}

A successful transaction means the user completes shopping and booking of ancillaries smoothly:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.

✅ **ServiceList** successfully add paid or free services to the order.

✅ **SeatAvailability** successfully add paid or free seats to the order.

✅ **OrderCreate** successfully adds the services, providing an immediate confirmation with a ticket for flight, EMD-for paid seat and paid ancillaries if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked examples on Shopping and booking with Seat and Ancillary which does not require pricing](https://drive.google.com/open?id=1uwmAqCLP8vl4KibEqbEqQQSQ5rDfLmNC)

###### 

## 

## Shopping and booking with Seat and Ancillary which requires pricing {#shopping-and-booking-with-seat-and-ancillary-which-requires-pricing}

#### 

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-6}

The user can shop for services like Excess baggage,  Lounge Access etc., using **VDC ServiceList API.** Seat can be selected using **VDC SeatAvailability API**. Once the required services and seats are selected, they must be priced using the **VDC FlightPrice API** before proceeding with booking.

###### 

#### Process {#process-6}

**AirShoppingRQ**

The user initiates a **one way search** for flights via the VDC system. The  VDC AirShoppingRQ for one way search for one PAX will look like below:

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {		"OriginDestinations": {			"OriginDestination": \[				{					"Departure": {						"AirportCode": {							"value": "CDG"						},						"Date": "2025-04-29"					},					"Arrival": {						"AirportCode": {							"value": "LHR"						}					},					"OriginDestinationKey": "OD1"				}			\]		}	},	"Preference": {		"FarePreferences": {			"Types": {				"Type": \[					{						"Code": "PUBL"					}				\]			}		}	},	"ResponseParameters": {		"ShopResultPreference": "OPTIMIZED",		"SortOrder": \[...\]	}} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. The FlightPriceRQ for one way search for one PAX will look like below:

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"Query": {		"OriginDestination": \[			{				"Flight": \[...\]			}		\],		"Offers": {			"Offer": \[				{					"OfferID": {						"value": "b2545361-654e-407e-9453-61654ec00001",						"Owner": "26",						"Channel": "NDC"					},					"OfferItemIDs": {						"OfferItemID": \[							{								"value": "a23aac53-6653-4ca4-baac-5366539ca497",								"refs": \[									"26-PAX1"								\]							}						\]					}				}			\]		}	},	"DataLists": {...},	"ShoppingResponseID": {		"ResponseID": {			"value": "6GJmkiaCYz1GgDVuOK1nXZfWu6UTfh5QBtwQHysxiWg-26"		}	}} |
| :---- |

**ServiceList:**

Once the user selects the priced offer from the VDC FlightPriceRS, the VDC ServiceListRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. The ServiceListRQ for one way for one PAX search will look like below:

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"ObjectKey": "26-PAX1",						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"Query": {		"OriginDestination": \[			{				"Flight": \[					{						"SegmentKey": "SEG1",						"Departure": {							"AirportCode": {								"value": "CDG"							},							"Date": "2025-04-29T07:35:00.000",							"Time": "07:35"						},						"Arrival": {							"AirportCode": {								"value": "LHR"							},							"Date": "2025-04-29T08:00:00.000",							"Time": "08:00"						}					}				\]			}		\],		"Offers": {			"Offer": \[				{					"OfferID": { *//Offer details from FlightPriceRS*						"ObjectKey": "b2545361-654e-407e-9453-61654ec00001",						"value": "b2545361-654e-407e-9453-61654ec00001",						"Owner": "26",						"Channel": "NDC"					},					"OfferItemIDs": {						"OfferItemID": \[							{								"value": "a23aac53-6653-4ca4-baac-5366539ca497"							}						\]					}				}			\]		}	},	"ShoppingResponseID": {		"ResponseID": {			"value": "6GJmkiaCYz1GgDVuOK1nXZfWu6UTfh5QBtwQHysxiWg-26"*//From FlightPriceRS*		}	}} |
| :---- |

Mapping Document

| FlightPriceRS | ServiceListRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList/AnonymousTraveler/ObjectKey | ServiceListRQ/Travelers/Traveler/AnonymousTraveler/ObjectKey |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList/AnonymousTraveler/PTC/value | ServiceListRQ/Travelers/Traveler/AnonymousTraveler/PTC/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/SegmentKey  | ServiceListRQ/Query/OriginDestination/Flight/SegmentKey |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/AirportCode/value | ServiceListRQ/Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Departure/AirportCode/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/Date | ServiceListRQ/Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Departure/Date |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/Time | ServiceListRQ/Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Departure/Time |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Arrival/AirportCode/value | ServiceListRQ/Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Arrival/AirportCode/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Arrival/Terminal/Name | ServiceListRQ/Query/OriginDestination/Flight/Arrival/Terminal/Name |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferID/value | ServiceListRQ/Query/Offers/Offer/OfferID/value |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferID/Owner  | ServiceListRQ/Query/Offers/Offer/OfferID/Owner |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferPrice/OfferItemID | ServiceListRQ/Query/Offers/Offer/OfferItemIDs/OfferItemID/value |
| FlightPriceRS/ShoppingResponseID/ResponseID/value | ServiceListRQ/ShoppingResponseID/ResponseID/value |

**SeatAvailability:**

The VDC **SeatAvailability API** allows users to search for seats per passenger based on the availability from the airline. The API has the ability to retrieve seat maps for one or more flight segments.

The SeatAvailabilityRQ for one way for one PAX search will look like below:

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"ObjectKey": "26-PAX1",						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"Query": {		"OriginDestination": \[			{				"FlightSegmentReference": \[					{						"ref": "SEG1"					}				\]			}		\],		"Offers": {			"Offer": \[				{					"OfferID": { *//Offer details from FlightPriceRS*						"ObjectKey": "b2545361-654e-407e-9453-61654ec00001",						"value": "b2545361-654e-407e-9453-61654ec00001",						"Owner": "26",						"Channel": "NDC"					},					"OfferItemIDs": {						"OfferItemID": \[							{								"value": "a23aac53-6653-4ca4-baac-5366539ca497"							}						\]					}				}			\]		}	},	"DataLists": {		"FlightSegmentList": {			"FlightSegment": \[				{					"SegmentKey": "SEG1",					"Departure": {						"AirportCode": {							"value": "CDG"						},						"Date": "2025-04-29T00:00:00.000",						"Time": "07:35"					},					"Arrival": {						"AirportCode": {							"value": "LHR"						},						"Date": "2025-04-29T00:00:00.000",						"Time": "08:00"					}				}			\]		},		"FareList": {			"FareGroup": \[				{					"ListKey": "26-FG-VYSFBNLA-70J",					"FareBasisCode": {						"Code": "VYSFBNLA"					}				}			\]		}	},	"ShoppingResponseID": {		"ResponseID": {			"value": "6GJmkiaCYz1GgDVuOK1nXZfWu6UTfh5QBtwQHysxiWg-26" *//From FlightPriceRS*		}	}} |

**FlightPrice for pricing ancillary and seat**

If the PricedInd is **false** for the selected offer for ancillaries and seat, the ancillaries and seat along with the flight item has to be priced using the VDC FlightPriceRQ API before proceeding with the booking. While pricing, ensure that the required quantity for each ancillary offer is accurately specified.

**OrderCreate:**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. 

A sample One way OrderCreateRQ for One PAX would look like below:

#### Success {#success-6}

A successful transaction means the user completes a one-way flight booking with **pricing and** **addition of both seat and ancillaries** smoothly:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **ServiceList** successfully provides offers for paid or free services that can be added  to the order. 

✅ **SeatAvailability** successfully provides offers for paid or free seats that can be added to the order.

✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare of the ancillary and seat selected, ensuring transparency in pricing. Read more details in the [Concept](#pricedind) Section.

✅ **OrderCreate** successfully adds the services, providing an immediate confirmation with an EMD-ticket in the response OrderViewRS if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked examples on one way shop to book with seat and ancillaries which requires pricing](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1w0xiPFZvvSDueG44W17LBRD58MJRtA5o?usp=drive_link)

In addition to the combined shopping booking of seats and ancillaries together (where pricing is required), the following scenarios are also commonly encountered and must be handled by the API customer:

**Shopping and booking with Seat which requires pricing**

Definition of use case

Users can shop for available seats using the **VDC SeatAvailability API**. If the selected seat requires pricing, it must be priced using the **VDC FlightPriceRQ API** before proceeding with the booking.

Success

A successful transaction means the traveler completes a booking with the **seat priced and added** correctly to the order.

[Click here to view worked examples on one way shop to book with seat which requires pricing](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1BbaT0HduXrJ7pToA8LoqdpsaqiK9jad-?usp=drive_link)

**Shopping and booking with Ancillary which requires pricing**

Definition of use case

Users can shop for additional ancillaries (e.g., baggage, lounge access) using the **VDC ServiceList API**. If the selected ancillary requires pricing, it must be priced using the **VDC FlightPriceRQ API** before proceeding with the booking.

Success

A successful transaction means the traveler completes a booking with **the priced ancillary added** correctly to the order.

[Click here to view worked examples on one way shop to book with ancillary which requires pricing](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1hM0dEys4B_Ze8Bmt5_mIS2ix1L29YIke?usp=drive_link)

**Shopping and booking with Seat and Ancillary where either one of them requires pricing**

Definition of use case

Users can shop for ancillary services like Excess Baggage, Lounge Access etc using the **VDC ServiceList API**, while seat selection is done through the **VDC SeatAvailability API**. If either the selected seat or ancillary service requires pricing, the specific item only must be priced using the **VDC FlightPriceRQ API** before proceeding with the booking. The item that doesn’t require pricing should be passed directly in the **OrderCreate API**.

Success

A successful transaction means the traveler completes a booking **with both services (priced or unpriced as applicable) added** correctly to the order,

[Click here to view worked examples on one way shop to book with seat and ancillaries where either of the one requires pricing](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1X6AFuCInHO5i82tmR0OxzD47kIbAAhXN?usp=drive_link)

## Shop using FQTV {#shop-using-fqtv}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-7}

This use case enables searching for a specific FQTV (Frequent Flyer Number); the user's frequent flyer program details are provided to the airline to receive personalized offers, benefits, or fare adjustments based on their loyalty status with the airline. 

#### Process {#process-7}

A search request with an FQTV must include a valid Loyalty Program ID for a specific airline, along with other mandatory search parameters. FQTV has to be provided in all the requests from AirShopping to OrderCreate in Prime booking.

**AirShopping**

The traveler searches with the Loyalty Programme information (FQTV/FFID) in the search request.

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"RecognizedTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						},						"Name": {							"Title": "MR",							"Given": \[								{									"value": "ALLEN"								}							\],							"Surname": {								"value": "PAUL"							}						},						"FQTVs": \[							{								"Account": {									"Number": {										"value": "IN2650D01"									}								},								"AirlineID": {									"value": "BA"								}							}						\]					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {....},	"Preference": {....},	"ResponseParameters": {....}} |
| :---- |

**ServiceList**

**SeatAvailability**

Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ must be constructed using the FQTV details and other required fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ.

**OrderCreate**

Upon receiving a successful FlightPriceRS, SeatAvailability and/or ServiceList, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by providing FQTV details along with other necessary fields, such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId, and passenger information. A sample one-way OrderCreateRQ would appear as follows:
