**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with FQTV information added.

#### Success {#success-7}

A successful transaction means that the traveler completes a **flight booking for the requested FQTV**  with all elements confirmed:

✅ AirShopping retrieves all relevant flight offers with a RecognisedTravellerList in response at the DataList to airlines that provide information about the Frequent Flyer Travel Number being applied. If the API returns an Anonymous Traveller List, then the Frequent Flyer Travel Voucher requested is not applied.

✅ **FlightPrice** returns with RecognisedTravellerList in the response at the DataList.

✅ **ServiceList** successfully add paid or free services to the order.

✅ **SeatAvailability** successfully add paid or free seats to the order.

✅ **OrderCreate** successfully adds the services, providing an immediate confirmation with a ticket for flight, EMD-for paid seat and paid ancillaries if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked examples on FQTV](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/19jUNy7hnmlJSoCnVi6EM1JtG3kNG_CB6?usp=sharing)

## 

## Shop using account code(deal code or corporate code) {#shop-using-account-code(deal-code-or-corporate-code)}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-8}

This use case enables the search for a specific Account Code (Deal Code). Airlines provide deal codes to agencies to obtain curated offers, benefits, or fare adjustments.

#### Process {#process-8}

A search request containing a Deal Code specifies the Deal Code to be submitted to a specific airline, along with the other required search parameters. Deal Code has to be provided in all the requests from AirShopping to OrderCreate in Prime booking.

**AirShopping**

The traveler searches with the DealCode information in the search request.

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {		"OriginDestinations": {			"OriginDestination": \[....\]		}	},	"Preference": {....},	"ResponseParameters": {....},	"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "EK/ITXXX”//For multiple DealCodes the DealCode can be given here separated with commas			}		}	}} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

Once the traveler selects an offer from the AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ must be constructed with the DealCode details, along with other required mandatory elements such as the OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. A sample snippet for the VDC FlightPriceRQ is provided below.:

| {    "Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[....\]			}		\]	},    "Query": {        "OriginDestination": \[....\],        "Offers": {            "Offer": \[....\]        }    },	*"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "EK/ITXXX”//For multiple DealCodes the DealCode can be given here separated with commas			}		}	}*,    "DataLists": {        "AnonymousTravelerList": {			"AnonymousTraveler": \[				{					"ObjectKey": "EK-T1",					"PTC": {						"value": "ADT"					}				}			\]		}    },    "ShoppingResponseID": {        "Owner": "EK",        "ResponseID": {            "value": "3dQ4XSHhZ1LDG5MyEEJ3ldQfpYG5U9IvXAjsWUtEpUQ-EK"        }    }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with Deal Code information given.

**ServiceList**

Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC ServiceListRQ must be constructed using the DealCode details and other required fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC ServiceListRQ

| {	"Travelers": {....},	"Query": {....},     "Party": {....},     "ShoppingResponseID": {....},     "Metadata": {.....},      "Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": *"EK/ITXXX”//For multiple DealCodes the DealCode can be given here separated with commas*			}		}	},} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with Deal Code information given.

**SeatAvailability**

Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ must be constructed using the DealCode details and other required fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ.

| {  "DataLists": {....},  "Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": *"EK/ITXXX”//For multiple DealCodes the DealCode can be given here separated with commas*			}		}	},  "Query": {....},  "Travelers": {....},  "ShoppingResponseID": {....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with Deal Code information given.

**OrderCreate**

After receiving a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by including the DealCode details along with other required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId, and passenger details. A sample one-way OrderCreateRQ would look like this:

| {	"Query": {		"Passengers": {....},		"OrderItems": {....},		"DataLists": {....},		"Metadata": {			"PassengerMetadata": \[....\]		},		"Payments": {....},	*"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "EK/ITXXX"//For multiple DealCodes the DealCode can be given here separated with commas			}		}	}*} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with Deal Code information given.

#### Success {#success-8}

A successful transaction means that the traveler completes a **flight booking for the requested Deal code** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** will give the associated offers applied to the Deal Code in the request. Depending up on the Airline the requested account code will be present in the AirShoppingRS at AirShoppingRS/OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/PricedOffer/OfferPrice/FareDetail/FareComponent/FareRules/CorporateFare/Account/value and at AirShoppingRS/DataLists/FareList/FareGroup/Fare/FareDetail/Remarks/Remark/value, Kindly check the swagger for remark values.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the fare priced using the account code, similarly in FlightPriceRS the Remarks will be given at FlightPriceRS/FareList/FareGroup/Fare/FareDetail/Remarks/Remark/value  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight with the account code fare, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate)

[Click here to view worked examples on DealCode](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1vSZqsvVXneYHhmJFhmsWg1DV6_R_GAwx?usp=sharing)

## Shop using CLID {#shop-using-clid}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-9}

This use case enables shopping for flights by providing a **CLID (Corporate ID)**, which is used to identify the **corporate entity or organization** for which the booking is being made. The CLID allows the airline to recognize the associated corporate travel program and return offers based on **contracted fares**, **corporate benefits**, or **negotiated agreements** specific to that organization.

The goal is to apply corporate-specific pricing and policies during the offer generation process, ensuring that travelers booking on behalf of a company receive the appropriate fare structures and inclusions.

#### Process {#process-9}

A search request that includes a CLID specifies the airline to receive it, along with the other necessary search parameters.

**AirShopping**  
The traveler searches with the CLID information in the search request at AirShoppingRQ/Party/Sender/CorporateSender/CorporateCode

| {	"Travelers": { 		"Traveler": \[ 			{ 				"AnonymousTraveler": \[ 					{ 						"PTC": { 							"value": "ADT" 						} 					} 				\] 			} 		\] 	},	"CoreQuery": {		"OriginDestinations": {			"OriginDestination": \[....\]		}	},	"Preference": {....},	"ResponseParameters": {....}, 	"Party": { 		"Sender": { 			"CorporateSender": { 				"CorporateCode": "AT//NETXXX" 			} 		} 	} } |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

When a traveler selects a specific offer from the AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ must be constructed using the CLID details at \<FlightPriceRQ/Party/Sender/CorporateSender/CorporateCode, along with other necessary mandatory elements such as the OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is a sample snippet of the VDC FlightPriceRQ:

| {    "Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT",							"Quantity": 1						}					}				\]			}		\]	},    "Query": {        "OriginDestination": \[....\],        "Offers": {            "Offer": \[....\]        }    },	"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "AF//NETXXX"			}		}	},    "DataLists": {....},    "ShoppingResponseID": {        "Owner": "AF",        "ResponseID": {            "value": "3dQ4XSHhZ1LDG5MyEEJ3ldQfpYG5U9IvXAjsWUtEpUQ-EK"        }    }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with CLID information added.

**ServiceList**

Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC ServiceListRQ must be constructed using the CLID details and other required fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC ServiceListRQ

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT",							"Quantity": 1						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"Query": {....},	"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "AF//NETXXX"			}		}	},  "ShoppingResponseID": {....},	"Metadata": {.....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with CLID information added.

**SeatAvailability**  
Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ must be constructed using the CLID details and other required fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ.

| {  "DataLists": {....},  "Query": {....},  "Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "AF//NETXXX"			}		}	},  	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT",							"Quantity": 1						}					}				\]			}		\]	},  "ShoppingResponseID": {....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with CLID information added.

**OrderCreate**

After a successful FlightPriceRS is received, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by including the CLID details at \<OrderCreateRQ/Party/Sender/CorporateSender/CorporateCode\> along with other required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId, and passenger details. Below is a sample Oneway OrderCreateRQ:

| {	"Query": {		"Passengers": {			"Passenger": \[				{					"ObjectKey": "T1",					"PTC": {						"value": "ADT"					},					"Name": {						"Surname": {							"value": "PAUL"						},						"Given": \[							{								"value": "ALLEN"							}						\],						"Title": "Mr"					},					"AdditionalRoles": {						"PaymentContactInd": true					},					"Contacts": {						"Contact": \[							{								"AddressContact": {									"Street": \[										"Thapasya Building, 3rd ",										"Floor, Infopark Campus"									\],									"CityName": "Cochin",									"CountrySubDivisionCode": "",									"PostalCode": "673328",									"CountryCode": {										"value": "IN"									}								},								"EmailContact": {									"Address": {										"value": "test@verteil.com"									}								},								"PhoneContact": {									"Application": "Home",									"Number": \[										{											"value": "9876543210",											"CountryCode": "91"										}									\]								}							}						\]					},					"Age": {						"BirthDate": {							"value": "2019-06-04"						}					},					"Gender": {						"value": "Male"					}				}			\]		},		"OrderItems": {			"ShoppingResponse": {				"Owner": "AF",				"Offers": {					"Offer": \[....\]				},				"ResponseID": {					"value": "6DxYHYe0VjobLNXPwUw9xs3q3dbtWYkGnRL0CgEhQSQ-AF"				}			},			"OfferItem": \[....\]		},"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "AF//NETXXX"			}		}	},		"DataLists": {			"FareList": {				"FareGroup": \[....\]			}		},		"Metadata": {			"PassengerMetadata": \[....\]		},		"Payments": {....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with CLID information added.

#### Success {#success-9}

A successful transaction means that the traveler completes a **one-way flight booking for the requested CLID** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers with the requested CLID for a round-trip request  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate)

[Click here to view worked examples on CLID](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/179t-BTk2XXNEG9HRLELPUpa0MjAKaNZL?usp=sharing)

## Shop for negotiated and Private fares {#shop-for-negotiated-and-private-fares}

#### Definition of use case  This use case enables searching for a specific fare type. The fare types can be: {#definition-of-use-case-this-use-case-enables-searching-for-a-specific-fare-type.-the-fare-types-can-be:}

* Inclusive Tour (IT)   
  * *Contract Bulk (CB)*   
  * *Marine (MR)*   
  * *Student (STU)*   
  * *Humanitarian (HR)*   
  * *Visiting Friends & Relatives (VFR)*   
  * *Labor (LBR)*   
  * *Cruise (CRU)* 

  The fare type determines the pricing, fare rules, and availability of flight options. When making a request, you need to provide specific parameters, including the origin, destination, departure date, return date, and preferred fare type. The API will then return flight options along with fares that match your selected fare type. Users can choose one or more fare types in a single request based on their preferences.


  Negotiated (Nego) and Private (PVT) fares are determined by specific market and airline behaviors, which may include combinations such as:

  \- fare type

  \- fare type \+ deal code

  \- fare type \+ deal code \+ CLID  
      etc.

#### Process {#process-10}

A search request with a preferred fare type includes a Fare preference type in the request along with other mandatory search parameters:

**AirShopping**

The user searches using **Fare Type** as the only preference

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {		"OriginDestinations": {			"OriginDestination": \[....\]		}	},	"Preference": {		*"FarePreferences": {			"Types": {				"Type": \[					{						"Code": "IT"					}				\]			}		}*	},	"ResponseParameters": {....}} |
| :---- |

The traveler searches using **Fare type and Deal code**

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {		"OriginDestinations": {			"OriginDestination": \[....\]		}	},	"Preference": {		"FarePreferences": {			"Types": {				"Type": \[					{						"Code": "IT"					}				\]			}		}	},	"ResponseParameters": {....},"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "EK/ITXXX"			}		}	}} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

Once the traveler selects a particular offer from the AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID, and Pax details for the offer that was selected. 

FlightPriceRQ with using **only Fare type** will be look like a normal and the FlightPriceRQ search using both Fare Type and Deal code will look like:

| {    "Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT",							"Quantity": 1						}					}				\]			}		\]	},    "Query": {        "OriginDestination": \[....\],        "Offers": {            "Offer": \[                {                    "OfferID": {....},                    "OfferItemIDs": {                        "OfferItemID": \[....\]                    },                    "refs": \[                        {                            "Ref": "Reference\_key"//Reference\_key for augmentation                        }                    \]                }            \]        }    },	"Party": {		"Sender": {			"CorporateSender": {				"CorporateCode": "EK/ITXXX"			}		}	},    "DataLists": {....},    "Metadata": {        "Other": {            "OtherMetadata": \[                {                    "PriceMetadatas": {                        "PriceMetadata": \[                            {                                "MetadataKey": "{{Reference\_key}}",// Metadata informations associated with the reference key                                "AugmentationPoint": {                                    "AugPoint": \[....\]                                }                            }                        \]                    }                }            \]        }    }    "ShoppingResponseID": {....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping document for normal booking .Additionally the Reference Key and the associated Metadata has to be provided:

| AirShoppingRS | FlightPriceRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| *AirShoppingRS/OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/refs/Ref* | *FlightPriceRQ/Query/Offer/refs/Ref* |
| *AirShoppingRS/OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/refs/Ref* | *FlightPriceRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey* |
| *AirShoppingRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas* | *FlightPriceRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey/AugmentationPoint/AugPoint* |

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields, such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId, and pax details. A sample Oneway OrderCreateRQ would look like below:

| {	"Query": {		"Passengers": {			"Passenger": \[...\]		},		"OrderItems": {....},		"DataLists": {....}		},		"Metadata": {....},		"Payments": {			"Payment": \[				{					"Method": {						"Cash": {							"CashInd": "true"						}					},					"Amount": {						"Code": "INR",						"value": XXXX					}				}			\]		}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking.

For more details regarding the implementation of Negotiated Fares, please refer [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1H1CYnh3edmiuVFqR5bensE1HrGbdI0KwiY1AHI1R6Ns/edit?tab=t.0#heading=h.ms0xq4n6mdbc)

#### Success {#success-10}

A successful transaction means that the traveler completes a **flight booking for the requested fare type** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** gathers all relevant flight offers that match the requested fare type for a given request. The identifiable FareType Code for each returned offer can be found in AirShoppingRS/DataLists/FareList.FareGroup/Fare/FareDetail/Remarks/Remark/value. The corresponding codes for each fare type are available at [https://vdc-schema.stage.verteil.com/v1.](https://vdc-schema.stage.verteil.com/v1)

✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare at FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FareList/FareGroup/Fare/FareDetail/Remarks/Remark/value, ensuring transparency in pricing.

✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate)

[Click here to view worked examples on NegoFare](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1WK8mWth2lbWS9j7hFmXIdXSj-61PMOzx?usp=sharing)

## Shop using promocode {#shop-using-promocode}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-10}

This use case enables shopping for flights by applying a promo code, which is a predefined alphanumeric code issued by an airline to offer special discounts, exclusive fares, or limited-time deals. The promo code is provided during the search or pricing request to influence the returned offers.

When a promo code is submitted, it is validated against the airline's promotion engine, and if eligible, personalized or discounted fares are returned in the offer response. This use case supports marketing campaigns, targeted customer promotions, or partner-driven offers by allowing passengers to redeem benefits directly during the shopping phase.

#### Process {#process-11}

A search request with a PromoCode must include a valid PromoCode for a specific airline, typically for a specific route, along with other required search parameters.

**AirShopping**

The traveler must search with the Promo code in the search request. For aggregator search when multiple airlines are sent the PromoCode can be sent in the Array AirShoppingRQ/Qualifier/ProgramQualifiers/ProgramQualifier with Coma separated.

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {....},	"Preference": {....},	"ResponseParameters": {....},"Qualifier": {		"ProgramQualifiers": {			"ProgramQualifier": \[				{					"DiscountProgramQualifier": {						"Account": {							"value": "XXXX"						},						"AssocCode": {							"value": "BA"						},						"Name": {							"value": "PROMOCODE"						}					},                            {//For multiple PromoCodes, the codes could be sent here with Coma separation} 			\]		}	}} |
| :---- |

**FlightPrice**

When the traveler chooses a specific offer from the AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ must be constructed using the promo code and other necessary fields, such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and the passenger details for the selected offer. Here is an example snippet of the VDC FlightPriceRQ:

| {    "Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT",							"Quantity": 1						}					}				\]			}		\]	},    "Query": {....},    "DataLists": {....},    "ShoppingResponseID": {....},   "Qualifier": {		"ProgramQualifiers": {			"ProgramQualifier": \[				{					"DiscountProgramQualifier": {						"Account": {							"value": "XXXX"						},						"AssocCode": {							"value": "BA"						},						"Name": {							"value": "PROMOCODE"						}					}				}			\]		}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping document for normal booking, with Promo code informations given

**ServiceList**

Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC ServiceListRQ must be constructed with promo code and other necessary fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC ServiceListRQ

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"AnonymousTraveler": \[					{						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT",							"Quantity": 1						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"Query": {....},	"Party": {....},  "ShoppingResponseID": {....},    "Qualifier": {		"ProgramQualifiers": {			"ProgramQualifier": \[				{					"DiscountProgramQualifier": {						"Account": {							"value": "XXXX"						},						"AssocCode": {							"value": "BA"						},						"Name": {							"value": "PROMOCODE"						}					}				}			\]		}	"Metadata": {.....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with Promo code information given.

**SeatAvailability**  
Once the traveler selects a specific offer from the FlightPriceRS, the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ must be constructed using the  promo code and other necessary fields such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and passenger details for the selected offer. Below is an example snippet of the VDC SeatAvailabilityRQ.

| {  "DataLists": {....},  "Query": {....},  "Party": {....},  	"Travelers": {....},  "ShoppingResponseID": {....},  "Qualifier": {		"ProgramQualifiers": {			"ProgramQualifier": \[				{					"DiscountProgramQualifier": {						"Account": {							"value": "XXXX"						},						"AssocCode": {							"value": "BA"						},						"Name": {							"value": "PROMOCODE"						}					}				}			\]		}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping documents for normal booking, with CLID information given.

**OrderCreate**

After receiving a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed with other necessary fields, such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId, and passenger information. A sample one-way OrderCreateRQ would be as follows:

| {	"Query": {		"Passengers": {....},		"OrderItems": {....},		"DataLists": {....},		"Metadata": {....},		"Payments": {....},            "Qualifier": {		   "ProgramQualifiers": {			"ProgramQualifier": \[				{					"DiscountProgramQualifier": {						"Account": {							"value": "XXXX"						},						"AssocCode": {							"value": "BA"						},						"Name": {							"value": "PROMOCODE"						}					}				}			\]		}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping document for normal booking, with Promo code informations given

#### Success {#success-11}

A successful transaction means that the traveler completes a **flight booking for the requested Promo code**  with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers with the requested PromoCode for a round-trip request  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate)

[Click here to view worked examples on PromoCode](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1Z1orM88e4ByKZinKnQ9i8zImZW3axK2p?usp=sharing)

## Shop using business reward {#shop-using-business-reward}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-11}

This use case enables shopping for flights using a Business Reward program, where a passenger is associated with a corporate or business loyalty account. The business reward ID or corporate account identifier is included in the shopping request, allowing the airline to return offers that are tailored to the traveler’s corporate travel program, including negotiated fares, exclusive discounts, or contracted benefits.

The airline’s pricing engine evaluates the business reward credentials during the offer generation process and applies relevant fare rules, discounts, or bundled services agreed upon between the airline and the corporation. This use case supports corporate travel management, ensuring employees access eligible corporate fares and travel perks automatically during booking.

#### Process {#process-12}

A search request with a Business reward code must include a valid Business reward code for a specific airline, typically for a specific route, along with other required search parameters.

**AirShopping**

The traveler must search with the Business reward code in the search request.

| {	"Travelers": {		"Traveler": \[			{				"RecognizedTraveler": \[					{						"FQTVs": \[							{								"Account": {									"Number": {										"value": "XXXXX"//Business Reward Number									}								},								"AirlineID": {									"value": "AF"								},								"ProgramID": "Business"							}						\],						"PTC": {							"value": "ADT"						}					}				\]			}		\]	},	"CoreQuery": {....},	"Preference": {....},	"ResponseParameters": {....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping document for normal booking, with Business Reward informations given

**FlightPrice**

When the traveler chooses a specific offer from the AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ must be constructed using the promo code and other necessary fields, such as OfferID, OfferItemID, and the passenger details for the selected offer. Here is an example snippet of the VDC FlightPriceRQ:

| {    "Travelers": { 		"Traveler": \[ 			{ 				"RecognizedTraveler": \[ 					{ 						"PTC": { 							"value": "ADT", 							"Quantity": 1 						}, 						"Name": { 							"Title": "", 							"Given": \[ 								{ 									"value": "" 								} 							\], 							"Surname": { 								"value": "" 							} 						}, 						"FQTVs": \[ 							{ 								"Account": { 									"Number": { 										"value": "NETFLIX" 									} 								}, 								"AirlineID": { 									"value": "AF" 								}, 								"ProgramID": "Business" 							} 						\] 					} 				\] 			} 		\] 	} ,    "Query": {....},    "DataLists": {"RecognizedTravelerList": { 			"RecognizedTraveler": \[ 				{ 					"FQTVs": \[ 						{ 							"Account": { 								"Number": { 									"value": "NETFLIX" 								} 							}, 							"AirlineID": { 								"value": "AF" 							}, 							"ProgramID": "Business" 						} 					\], 					"PTC": { 						"value": "ADT" 					}, 					"ObjectKey": "AF-PAX1" 				} 			\] 		} },    "ShoppingResponseID": {        "Owner": "AF",        "ResponseID": {            "value": "3dQ4XSHhZ1LDG5MyEEJ3ldQfpYG5U9IvXAjsWUtEpUQ-AF"        }    }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping document for normal booking, with Business Reward informations given

**OrderCreate**

After receiving a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed with other necessary fields, such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId, and passenger information. A sample one-way OrderCreateRQ would be as follows:

| {	"Query": {		"Passengers": {			"Passenger": \[				{					"ObjectKey": "PAX1",					"PTC": {						"value": "ADT"					},					"Name": {						"Surname": {							"value": "PAUL"						},						"Given": \[							{								"value": "ALLEN"							}						\],						"Title": "Mr"					},					"FQTVs": {						"TravelerFQTV\_Information": \[							{								"AirlineID": {									"value": "AF"								},								"Account": {									"Number": {										"value": "NETFLIX"									}								},								"ProgramID": "Business"							}						\]					},					"AdditionalRoles": {						"PaymentContactInd": true					},					"Contacts": {						"Contact": \[							{								"AddressContact": {									"Street": \[										"Thapasya Building, 3rd ",										"Floor, Infopark Campus"									\],									"CityName": "Cochin",									"CountrySubDivisionCode": "",									"PostalCode": "673328",									"CountryCode": {										"value": "IN"									}								},								"EmailContact": {									"Address": {										"value": "test@verteil.com"									}								},								"PhoneContact": {									"Application": "Home",									"Number": \[										{											"value": "9876543210",											"CountryCode": "91"										}									\]								}							}						\]					},					"Age": {						"BirthDate": {							"value": "1978-09-08"						}					},					"Gender": {						"value": "Male"					}				}			\]		},		"OrderItems": {....},		"DataLists": {....},		"Metadata": {....},		"Payments": {....}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

Similar to mapping document for normal booking, with Business Reward informations given

#### Success {#success-12}

A successful transaction means that the traveler completes a **flight booking for the requested Business reward** with all elements confirmed:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flight offers with the Business reward programme  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate)

[Click here to view worked examples on Business Reward](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/13NEkS3_8nXq4ecjcDgwZb1HoJuKT5IQO?usp=sharing)

## 

## Pay for an order created without payment {#pay-for-an-order-created-without-payment}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-12}

The VDC **OrderCreate API** allows users to create the unpaid booking by utilizing the selected airline, route, offer details, and the passenger information provided. The API provides information on on-hold PNR/OrderID, which includes pricing details, booking status, and the time limits

* If a booking is created on hold it should be ticketed within the time limit..  
* The OrderViewRS of OrderCreate will return the PNR / OrderID if the transaction is a success.  
* Detailed description of time limits will be available under the Concepts section

#### Process {#process-13}

**AirShopping**

The traveler initiates a **one way search** for flights. 

**FlightPrice**    	

Once the traveller selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected.

**OrderCreate (Unpaid)**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. A sample Oneway OrderCreateRQ would look like below.

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below.

| {  "Query": {    "Filters": {      "OrderID": {        "value": "5G2VSN",        "Owner": "26",        "Channel": "NDC"      }    }  }}  |
| :---- |

| OrderViewRS | OrderRetrieveRQ |

| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Channel | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/Channel |

**OrderReshop**

The **IATA OrderReshopRQ** allows users, travel agencies, or third-party providers to request a **reprice** before confirming changes.

| {  "orderId": "5G2VSN",  "ownerCode": "26"} |
| :---- |

######

| OrderViewRS | OrderReShopRQ |

| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderReShopRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/ownerCode |

           "postalCode": "1234",            "cityName": "Kochi",            "countryName": "India",            "countryCode": "IN"          }        \],        "emailAddresses": \[          {            "emailAddressText": "abc.xyz@cc.com"          }        \]      }    }  },  "augmentations": {    "segmentDetails": {      "value": "rO0ABXNyADdjb20uZ29vZ2xlLnByb3RvYnVmLkdlbmVyYXRlZE1lc3NhZ2VMaXRlJFNlcmlhbGl6ZWRGb3JtAAAAAAAAAAACAANbAAdhc0J5dGVzdAACW0JMAAxtZXNzYWdlQ2xhc3N0ABFMamF2YS9sYW5nL0NsYXNzO0wAEG1lc3NhZ2VDbGFzc05hbWV0ABJMamF2YS9sYW5nL1N0cmluZzt4cHVyAAJbQqzzF/gGCFTgAgAAeHAAAABeClwqWgoPCgZvcmlnaW4SBRoDU0lOChQKC2Rlc3RpbmF0aW9uEgUaA0xIUgobCg1mYXJlQmFzaXNDb2RlEgoaCEUxMlNHT1BPChQKBGRhdGUSDBoKMjAyNS0wNC0wNXZyAB1jb20uZ29vZ2xlLnByb3RvYnVmLkxpc3RWYWx1ZQAAAAAAAAAAAgACQgAVbWVtb2l6ZWRJc0luaXRpYWxpemVkTAAHdmFsdWVzX3QAEExqYXZhL3V0aWwvTGlzdDt4cgAkY29tLmdvb2dsZS5wcm90b2J1Zi5HZW5lcmF0ZWRNZXNzYWdlAAAAAAAAAAECAAFMAA11bmtub3duRmllbGRzdAAlTGNvbS9nb29nbGUvcHJvdG9idWYvVW5rbm93bkZpZWxkU2V0O3hwdAAdY29tLmdvb2dsZS5wcm90b2J1Zi5MaXN0VmFsdWU=" **Mapping Document:** 

| OrderReShopRS | OrderChangeRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| response/repriceResult/noPriceChangeOrderItems/fareDetails/price/totalAmount | OrderChangeRQ/acceptRepricedOrder/ paymentFunctions/paymentProcessingDetails/amount |
| response/repriceResult/noPriceChangeOrderItems/fareDetails/price/totalAmount/currencyCode | OrderChangeRQ/acceptRepricedOrder/ paymentFunctions/paymentProcessingDetails/amount/currencyCode |
| response/repriceResult/noPriceChangeOrderItems/fareDetails/price/totalAmount/units | OrderChangeRQ/acceptRepricedOrder/ paymentFunctions/paymentProcessingDetails/amount/currencyCode/units |
| response/dataMap | OrderChangeRQ/acceptRepricedOrder/dataMap |
| response/dataMap/passengers | OrderChangeRQ/acceptRepricedOrder/dataMap/passengers |
| response/augmentations | OrderChangeRQ/augmentations |

#### Success {#success-13}

A successful transaction indicates that the traveller effectively completed the payment for an unpaid booking.

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅**Unpaid OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing a PNR & booking status  
✅ **OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, PNR.  
✅ **OrderReshop** allows to request a reprice before confirming changes.  
✅ **OrderChange** successfully will provide the ticket document in case of non LCC carriers which issues ticket along with the payments and order status. 

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1Zopl5GBbNa6cy5-Jf1dUDp_x9QmeYW29?usp=drive_link)

## 

## Retrieve an Order {#retrieve-an-order}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-13}

The **VDC OrderRetrieve API** enables users to retrieve their booking. The API provides all the details of the Order including Fare, Penalties, Baggage and Flight details will be returned in the OrderViewRS.

#### Process {#process-14}

**AirShopping**

The user initiates a **one way search** for flights via the VDC system. 

**FlightPrice**    	

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. A sample snippet for VDC FlightPriceRQ would look like below, 

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. OrderCreate RQ will be initiated and the user must have the capability to make a payment associated with the PNR, and upon successful completion of the transaction, both the payment status and the ticket will be provided in the response.

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below:

A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below.

| {  "Query": {    "Filters": {      "OrderID": {        "value": "5G2VSN",        "Owner": "26",        "Channel": "NDC"      }    }  }}  |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderViewRS | OrderRetrieveRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/Owner |

#### Success {#success-14}

A successful OrderRetrieve ensures that the user received full booking details without issues:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate). For other forms of payments, refer to the Payment Section.  
✅ **OrderRetrieve**successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, ticket number, PNR.

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1PPj4kuSFe4wabD3d9okdenpJ-v8U1KJs?usp=drive_link)

## 

## Re-issue/ Revalidation    {#re-issue/-revalidation}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-14}

The **VDC OrderReshop API** enables users to modify their current booking by reissuing it for a later date, changing the flights, upgrading the cabin etc. The API provides new offers and pricing information based on the requested changes and according to airline rules and policies.

#### Process {#process-15}

Modification with date change for one way single pax:

**AirShopping**

The user initiates a one way **search** for flights.

**FlightPrice**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. 

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. 

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below:

| {    "Query": {        "Filters": {            "OrderID": {                "value": "K1T2BP",                "Owner": "26",                "Channel": "NDC"            }        }    }} |
| :---- |

**OrderReshop (Reshop.V3)**

Once the order is retrieved, users can construct an OrderReshop RQ with OrderID, Owner details and origin destination details with the new departure date, cabin preference etc. The existing Orderitem has to be passed in this request.

| {	"ownerCode": "26",	"orderId": "K1T2BP",	"reshopOrder": {		"reshopOrderChoice": {			"serviceOrder": {				"addOfferItem": {					"responseControls": {...}						\]					},					"flightRequest": {						"originDestinationsCriteria": {							"originDestCriteria": \[								{ *//origin or destination can be changed according to airline policies*									"originStationCode": "DXB",									"destStationCode": "LHR",									"depDate": "2025-04-23",									"cabinPreferences": \[										"Y" *//Can be upgraded according to airline policies*									\]								}							\]						}					}				},				"deleteOrderItems": \[					{						"orderItemRefId": "P77686CC3-7073-45A8-9ED9b65a4bi3z8s0t-1-1" *//This refers to the existing OrderItem in the booking that will be replaced.*                                     "retainServiceRefIds": \[\] *//Reference to the service in the Order Item that the passenger wants to retain in the reissued offer.(can be used in case of partial reissue)* 					}				\]			}		}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderViewRS of OrderRetrieveRQ | OrderReshopRQ (Reshop.V3) |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderReshopRQ/orderId |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderReshopRQ/ownerCode |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/value | OrderReshopRQ/reshopOrder/reshopOrderChoice/serviceOrder/deleteOrderItems/orderItemRefId |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/Associations/IncludedService/ServiceReferences | OrderReshopRQ/reshopOrder/reshopOrderChoice/serviceOrder/deleteOrderItems/retainServiceRefIds |

**OrderReshop (Requote.V3)**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC OrderReshopRS(Reshop.V3), the VDC OrderReshopRQ(Requote.V3) has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected to price the new offer.   
The OrderReshopRQ for one way reissue for one PAX will look like below:

| {	"ownerCode": "26",	"orderId": "K1T2BP",	"requoteOrder": {		"selectedOffers": \[			{				"selectedOfferItems": \[					{						"offerItemRefId": "R9B-21F-4D20-A-2-1", *//new offerItem*						"paxRefIds": \[							"T1"						\],						"selectedServiceRefIds": \[							"R9EE5F7DB-21F-4D20-AF2D-2-1-1"						\]					}				\],				"shoppingResponseId": "R9EE5F7DB-21F-4D20-AF2D",				"offerRefId": "R9EE5F7DB-21F-4D20-AF2D-2"			}		\],		"affectedOrderItems": \[			{				"orderItemRefId": "P77686CC3-7073-45A8-9ED9b65a4bi3z8s0t-1-1" *//Original OrderItem*			}		\],		"passengers": {			"T1": {				"ptc": "ADT",				"individual": {					"birthdate": "1976-03-02",					"genderCode": "M",					"titleName": "MR",					"givenNames": \[						"JON"					\],					"surname": "DOE"				},				"contactInfoRefId": "CI1"			}		},		"augmentations": {...}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderReshopRS (Reshop.V3) | OrderReshopRQ (Requote.V3) |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/addOfferItems/offerItemId | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/selectedOffers/selectedOfferItems/offerItemRefId |
| OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/addOfferItems/reshopPriceAndFareDetails/differential/diff/fareDetails/passengerRefs | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/selectedOffers/selectedOfferItems/paxRefIds |
| OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/addOfferItems/offeredServices/serviceId | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/selectedOffers/selectedOfferItems/selectedServiceRefIds |
| OrderReshopRS/shoppingResponseId | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/selectedOffers/shoppingResponseId |
| OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/offerId | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/selectedOffers/offerRefId |
| OrderReshopRS/response/dataMap/passengers | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/passengers |
| OrderReshopRS/requoteOrder/augmentations | OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/augmentations |

**OrderChange (UpdateOrderItems.V3)**

Once the new offer is priced, the existing order can be updated with the new offer, and payment can be made for reissue/revalidation of the ticket using **OrderChangeRQ API**.

| {	"orderId": "K1T2BP",	"updateOrderItems": {		"acceptOffers": \[			{				"selectedPricedOffers": \[					{						"offerRefId": "R9B-21F-4D20-A-2-1", *//new Offer*						"ownerCode": "26",						"selectedOfferItems": \[							{								"offerItemRefId": "R9EE5F7DB-21F-4D20-AF2D-2", *//new OfferItem*								"paxRefIds": \[									"T1"								\]							}						\],						"shoppingResponseRefId": "R9EE5F7DB-21F-4D20-AF2D"					}				\]			}		\],		"paymentFunctions": \[			{				"paymentProcessingDetails": {					"typeCode": "CA",					"amount": {						"currencyCode": "INR" *//no amount in case of even exchange*					}				}			}		\],		"dataMap": {			"passengers": {				"T1": {					"ptc": "ADT",					"individual": {						"birthdate": "1976-03-02",						"genderCode": "M",						"titleName": "MR",						"givenNames": \[							"JON"						\],						"surname": "DOE"					},					"contactInfoRefId": "CI1"				}			},			"contactInfos": {				"CI1": {					"phone": \[						{							"contactType": "HOME",							"phoneNumber": "919999999999"						}					\],					"emailAddresses": \[						{							"emailAddressText": "ABC.XYZ@CC.COM"						}					\]				}			},			"paxSegments": {				"S2": {					"dep": {						"stationCode": "DXB",						"terminalName": "3",						"schedDateTime": "2025-04-25T02:35"					},					"arrival": {						"stationCode": "JFK",						"terminalName": "4",						"schedDateTime": "2025-04-25T08:15"					},					"aircraftType": "388",					"duration": "PT14H40M",					"marketingInfo": {						"carrierCode": "26",						"flightNumber": "203"					},					"operatingInfo": {						"carrierCode": "26"					}				}			},			"paxJourneys": {				"F2": {					"origin": "DXB",					"dest": "JFK",					"duration": "PT14H40M",					"paxSegmentRefIds": \[						"S2"					\]				}			},			"baggageAllowances": {...}			},			"priceClasses": {....}		},		"augmentations": {...}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderReshopRS(Requote.V3) | OrderChangeRQ (UpdateOrderItems.V3) |
| :---- | ----- |
| OrderReshopRS/response/requotedOffer/offerId | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/acceptOffers/selectedPricedOffers/offerRefId |
| OrderReshopRS/response/requotedOffer/ownerCode | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/acceptOffers/selectedPricedOffers/ownerCode |
| OrderReshopRS/response/requotedOffer/addOfferItems/offerItemId | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/acceptOffers/selectedPricedOffers/selectedOfferItems/offerItemRefId |
| OrderReshopRS/response/requotedOffer/addOfferItems/requoteDiffPriceAndFareDetails/diff/fareDetails/passengerRefs | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/acceptOffers/selectedPricedOffers/selectedOfferItems/paxRefIds |
| OrderReshopRS/response/shoppingResponseId | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/acceptOffers/selectedPricedOffers/shoppingResponseRefId |
| OrderReshopRS/response/dataMap/passengers | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/dataMap/passengers |
| OrderReshopRS/response/dataMap/contactInfos | OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/dataMap/contactInfos |

#### Success {#success-15}

A successful reissue ensures that the user will be able to successfully modify the itinerary as per the requirement.

✅ **OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, ticket number, OrderID.  
✅ **OrderReshop** successfully provide all the available offers for the requested change.Read more details in the Concept section [Concepts](#reshop-price-splitup)  
✅ **OrderRequote** will price the offer providing accurate fare details.  
✅ **OrderChange** successfully will have new offer issued with an EMD-ticket in the response OrderViewRS if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked examples on modify itinerary of one way shop](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1__zySx5ktVbQKFn7Vbe2eQWzVLvOrpD7?usp=drive_link)

######  

Modifications Supported by the **OrderReshop API** according to airline rules and policies are as follows:

1\. Partial itinerary Reissue with Date Change

*Definition of use case*  
The VDC **OrderReshop API** allows users to modify the itinerary by changing the departure date for either outbound or inbound journey of a round trip by retaining the other segment.

Success  
A successful reissue ensures that the user will be able to successfully modify the itinerary by changing the departure date of either one of the journeys of the itinerary.

[Click here to view worked examples on Partial itinerary reissue with date change](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1A3HvQbX2Ig0K1Tg6hS-XVMw-mAwRoCbT?usp=drive_link)

2\. Full itinerary Reissue with date change

*Definition of use case*  
The VDC **OrderReshop API** allows users to modify the whole itinerary by changing the departure date for both the outbound and inbound journey of a round trip.

Success  
A successful reissue ensures that the user will be able to successfully modify the itinerary by changing the departure date of both the journeys of the itinerary.

[Click here to view worked examples on modify itinerary by date change for whole itinerary](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1f6YHCZBq8UQ7hSz0NTr3pJGxjRTI9Lm5?usp=drive_link)

3\. Partial itinerary Reissue with Cabin upgrade

*Definition of use case*  
The VDC **OrderReshop API** allows users to modify the itinerary by changing the cabin preference for either outbound or inbound journey of a round trip by retaining the other segment.

Success  
A successful reissue ensures that the user will be able to successfully modify the itinerary by upgrading the cabin of either one of the journeys of the itinerary.

[Click here to view worked examples on modify itinerary by cabin upgrade for one journey](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1O2PLfDeORiqqntNcGJgrJCVNGftEX0sm?usp=drive_link)

4\. Full itinerary Reissue with Cabin upgrade 

*Definition of use case*  
The VDC **OrderReshop API** allows users to modify the itinerary by changing the cabin preference for both the outbound and inbound journey of a round trip.

Success  
A successful reissue ensures that the user will be able to successfully modify the itinerary by upgrading the cabin of both the journeys of the itinerary.

[Click here to view worked examples on modify itinerary by cabin upgrade for whole itinerary](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1S0ppAj0pmbz8NT5yB6ae77KutOt_esJp?usp=drive_link)

5\. Reissue with modifying the Origin or destination

*Definition of use case*  
The VDC **OrderReshop API** allows users to modify the itinerary by changing either the origin or destination of the inbound or outbound journey.

Success  
A successful reissue ensures that the user will be able to successfully modify the itinerary by changing either the Origin or the destination of the journey.

[Click here to view worked examples on modify itinerary by changing origin or destination of the journey](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1s16CpUmitBzj66BmUN4YFm-42xOVGGwe?usp=drive_link)

## 

## Cancellation of a paid booking. {#cancellation-of-a-paid-booking.}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-15}

The VDC **OrderCancel API** allows users to cancel passenger(s), one or many services (OrderItem), including flights and ancillaries (OrderItem) from Order.

If the order is paid we get a refundQuote while using ItinReshopRQ and then followed by an OrderCancel which will cancel the order.

#### Process {#process-16}

 **AirShopping**

The user initiates a **search** for flights via the VDC system. 

**FlightPrice**    	

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShoppingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected.

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. OrderCreate RQ will be initiated and the user must have the capability to make a payment associated with the PNR, and upon successful completion of the transaction, both the payment status and the ticket will be provided in the response

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. 

A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below.

| {  "Query": {    "Filters": {      "OrderID": {        "value": "JG1ZUF",        "Owner": "26",        "Channel": "NDC"      }    }  }} |
| :---- |

**ItinReshop(refundQuote):**

The **VDC ItinReshopRQ** enables the user to request for cancellation of the paid Order. The API provides cancellation with different outcomes in terms of refund and penalty.

A sample VDC ItinReshop RQ would look like below:

| {  "Query": {    "Reshop": {      "Actions": \[        {          "ActionType": {            "value": "Cancel” //Cancel action approved          },          "OrderID": {            "value": "JG1ZUF",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"          },          "OrderItems": {            "OrderItem": \[              {                "OrderItemID": {                  "value": "26\_JG1ZUF\_AIR-1",                  "Owner": "26"                }              }            \]          }        }      \]    }  }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

| OrderViewRS | ItinReshopRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderID |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderID/Owner |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Channel | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderID/Channel |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderItems |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderItems/OrderItem |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/value | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/Owner | ItinReshopRQ/Query/Reshop/Actions/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/Owner |

**OrderCancel:**

The user requests full cancellation of the paid order.

A sample VDC OrderCancel would look like below:

| {  "ExpectedRefundAmount": {    "Total": {      "value": 57675,      "Code": "INR"    }  },  "Query": {    "OrderID": \[      {        "value": "JG1ZUF",        "Owner": "26",        "Channel": "NDC"      }    \]  }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

| ItinReshop RS OrderViewRS | OrderCancelRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/ReShopOffers/ReShopOffer/ReshopDifferential/ReshopDue/ByAirline/Total | OrderCancelRQ/ExpectedRefundAmount/Total |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/ReShopOffers/ReShopOffer/ReshopDifferential/ReshopDue/ByAirline/Total/Amount/value | OrderCancelRQ/ExpectedRefundAmount/Total/value |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/ReShopOffers/ReShopOffer/ReshopDifferential/ReshopDue/ByAirline/Total/Amount/Code | OrderCancelRQ/ExpectedRefundAmount/Total/value/Code |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/Order/OrderID | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/ |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| ItinReshopRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/Owner |
| ItinReshopRS/CorrelationID | OrderCancelRQ/CorrelationID |
| ItinReshopRS/ReShoppingResponseID/ResponseID |  OrderCancelRQ/CorrelationID |

#### Success {#success-16}

A successful OrderCancel ensures that the user completes the cancellation and  receives the refund amount if any without any issues:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing a PNR & booking status  
✅**OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, PNR.

✅**ItinReshop** will provide cancellation with different outcomes in terms of refund & penalty. To know more about how refund is calculated refer to the document [ Cancellation Use Cases and Refund Handling in ItinReshopRS](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1sJP5p5LvUymvE9nipAPibTqkWF1B5MFQ7C7UzwEqQ-k/edit?usp=sharing)  
✅**OrderCancel** The paid order would be completely cancelled with a full refund if the request falls within the void window. If a penalty is imposed on the booking, the corresponding amount will be deducted, and the remaining balance will be refunded to the user 

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1WpUDD18mpez5NZtu1E33JlX6WnBQWbNe)

###### 

## 

## Cancellation for an unpaid booking {#cancellation-for-an-unpaid-booking-1}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-16}

The VDC **OrderCancel API** allows users to cancel passenger(s), one or many services (OrderItem), including flights and ancillaries (OrderItem) from Order.

If an order is unpaid, the entire order can be cancelled directly.

#### 

#### Process {#process-17}

 **AirShopping**

The user initiates a **one way search** for flights via the VDC system. 

**FlightPrice**    	

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected.

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. OrderCreate RQ will be initiated and an unpaid booking will be created with an on hold PNR.

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. 

A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below.

| {	"Query": {		"Filters": {			"OrderID": {				"Owner": "26",				"Channel": "NDC",				"value": "6K2D5J"			}		}	}} |
| :---- |

**OrderCancel:**

The user requests cancellation of the unpaid order.

A sample unpaid OrderCancelRQ would look like below:

| {  "Query": {    "OrderID": \[      {        "value": "6K2D5J",        "Owner": "26",        "Channel": "NDC"      }    \]  }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:**

| OrderViewRS | OrderCancelRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/ |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/Owner |

#### Success {#success-17}

A successful OrderCancel ensures that the user completes the cancellation.

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing a PNR & booking status  
✅**OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, PNR.

✅**ItinReshop** will provide cancellation with different outcomes in terms of refund & penalty  
✅**OrderCancel** The unpaid order would be completely cancelled.

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1V9rMI0x7P-k6O1Vq3pp2FND26hOZhw6x)

## Add seat which does not require pricing to an existing Order  {#add-seat-which-does-not-require-pricing-to-an-existing-order}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-17}

The **VDC SeatAvailability API** allows users to search for seats per passenger based on the availability from the airline. The API has the ability to retrieve seat maps for one or more flight segments. This service returns a priced seat map for the requested flight segments in a requested cabin.

#### Process {#process-18}

**AirShopping**

The user initiates a **search** for flights. 

**FlightPrice**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. 

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. 

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below,

| {  "Query": {    "Filters": {      "OrderID": {        "value": "ZXP1KY",         "Owner": "26",        "Channel": "NDC"      }    }  }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderViewRS | OrderRetrieveRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/Owner |

**Seat Availability**

The VDC **SeatAvailability API** allows users to search for seats per passenger based on the availability from the airline. The API has the ability to retrieve seat maps for one or more flight segments. This service returns a priced seat map for the requested flight segments for the booked cabin.  A sample SeatAvailabilityRQ would look like below,

| {  "Query": {    "OrderID": {      "value": "ZXP1KY",      "Owner": "26",      "Channel": "NDC"    }  }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderRetrieveRS | SeatAvailabilityRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderRetrieveRS/Order/OrderID/value | SeatAvailabilityRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| OrderRetrieveRS/Order/OrderID/Owner | SeatAvailabilityRQ/Query/OrderID/value/Owner |

#### Success {#success-18}

A successful transaction means the traveler completes adding a paid seat or free seat to the existing order.

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate). For other forms of payments, refer to the Payment Section.  
✅ **OrderRetrieve**successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, ticket number, PNR.  
✅ **SeatAvailability** successfully add paid or free seat to the order.Read more details in the Concept section [Concepts](#heading=h.58y65ic17ir7)  
✅ **OrderChange** successfully will have the seat, providing an immediate confirmation with an EMD-ticket if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1VVPJ3ocwxP4_ugg-atU3SUg4Bm45uVvZ)

## Add seat which requires pricing to an existing Order {#add-seat-which-requires-pricing-to-an-existing-order}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-18}

The **VDC SeatAvailability API** allows users to search for seats per passenger based on the availability from the airline. The API has the ability to retrieve seat maps for one or more flight segments. This service returns a seat map for the requested flight segments in a requested cabin without pricing. Hence requires a pricing call. 

#### Process {#process-19}

**AirShopping**

The user initiates a **return search** for flights via the VDC system. 

**FlightPrice**

Once the user selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. 

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. 

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieve request will look like below,

| {	"Query": {		"Filters": {			"OrderID": {				"value": "JZ2VRO",				"Owner": "26",				"Channel": "NDC"			}		}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderViewRS | OrderRetrieveRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | OrderRetrieveRQ/Query/Filters/OrderID/Owner |

**Seat Availability**

The VDC **SeatAvailability API** allows users to search for seats per passenger based on the availability from the airline. The API has the ability to retrieve seat maps for one or more flight segments. This service returns a priced seat map for the requested flight segments in a requested cabin.  A sample SeatAvailabilityRQ would look like below

| {	"Query": {		"OrderID": {			"value": "JZ2VRO",			"Owner": "26",			"Channel": "NDC"		}	}} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderRetrieveRS | SeatAvailabilityRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderRetrieveRS/Order/OrderID/value | SeatAvailabilityRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| OrderRetrieveRS/Order/OrderID/Owner | SeatAvailabilityRQ/Query/OrderID/value/Owner |

**FlightPrice**

Once the traveller selects a particular seat from the VDC SeatAvailabilityRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the seat that was selected.

| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/AirlineID/value | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/AirlineID/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/FlightNumber/value | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/FlightNumber/value |

#### Success {#success-19}

A successful transaction means the traveler completes adding a paid seat or free seat to the existing order.

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate).   
✅ **OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, ticket number, PNR.  
✅ **SeatAvailability** successfully add paid or free seat to the order.Read more details in the Concept section [Concepts](#pricedind)  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderChange** successfully will have the seat, providing an immediate confirmation with an EMD-ticket if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1jxQBrBTYBcRTyOLEukYYpdfvmxLa9qTN)

## Add ancillary which does not require pricing to an Existing Order {#add-ancillary-which-does-not-require-pricing-to-an-existing-order}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-19}

The **VDC ServiceList API** allows users to shop for services like Excess baggage, Lounge Access to add to the booking. The API returns available services, including their pricing details. 

#### Process {#process-20}

**AirShopping**

The traveler initiates a search for flights. 

**FlightPrice**

Once the traveller selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. 

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. 

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below,

| {    "Query": {        "Filters": {            "OrderID": {                "value": "1AVRFB",                "Owner": "26",                "Channel": "NDC"            }        }    }} |
| :---- |

**ServiceList**

Once the user retrieves the order, the VDC ServiceListRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. A sample ServiceListRQ would look like below,

| {    "Query": {        "OrderID": {            "value": "1AVRFB",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"        }    }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderViewRS | ServiceListRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | ServiceListRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | ServiceListRQ/Query/OrderID/Owner |

**OrderChange**

Once the user receives a successful ServiceListRS, the VDC OrderChangeRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. A sample OrderChangeRQ would look like below,

#### Success {#success-20}

A successful transaction means the traveler completes addition of the selected service to the booking:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the confirmed fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate).   
✅ **OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, ticket number, PNR.  
✅ **ServiceList** successfully add paid or free services to the order.Read more details in the [Concept](#concepts) Section.  
✅ **OrderChange** successfully adds the services, providing an immediate confirmation if the payment is successful.

## Add ancillary which requires pricing to an Existing Order {#add-ancillary-which-requires-pricing-to-an-existing-order}

#### Definition of use case {#definition-of-use-case-20}

The **VDC ServiceList API** allows users to shop for services like Excess baggage, Lounge Access, Priority Access, WIFI, Sports Equipment, Limousine (Chauffer Driver) to add to the booking. The API returns available services, excluding their pricing details. 

#### Process {#process-21}

**AirShopping**

The traveler initiates a search for flights. 

**FlightPrice**

Once the traveller selects a particular offer from the VDC AirShopingRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ has to be constructed using the OfferID, OfferItemID and Pax details for the offer that was selected. 

**OrderCreate**

Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderCreateRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. 

**OrderRetrieve**

Once the user receives a successful OrderViewRS, the VDC OrderRetrieve API is constructed by using the OrderID and the Owner details in the OrderCreateRS. A sample OrderRetrieveRQ would look like below,

| {    "Query": {        "Filters": {            "OrderID": {                "value": "TC2S3C",                "Owner": "26",                "Channel": "NDC"            }        }    }} |
| :---- |

**ServiceList**

Once the user retrieves the order, the VDC ServiceListRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. A sample ServiceListRQ would look like below,

| {    "Query": {        "OrderID": {            "value": "TC2S3C",            "Owner": "26",            "Channel": "NDC"        }    }} |
| :---- |

**Mapping Document:** 

| OrderViewRS | ServiceListRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/value | ServiceListRQ/Query/OrderID/value |
| OrderViewRS/Response/Order/OrderID/Owner | ServiceListRQ/Query/OrderID/Owner |

**FlightPrice**

Once the user receives a successful ServiceListRS, the VDC FlightPriceRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId and OfferItemRefId of the service selected. A sample FlightPriceRQ would look like below,

| {    "Travelers": {        "Traveler": \[            {                "AnonymousTraveler": \[                    {                        "PTC": {                            "value": "ADT"                        }                    }                \]            }        \]    },    "Query": {        "OriginDestination": \[            {                "Flight": \[                    {                        "SegmentKey": "SEG1",                        "Departure": {                            "AirportCode": {                                "value": "DOH"             "AirportCode": {                                "value": "LHR"                            },                            "Date": "2025-04-03T06:25:00.000", "MarketingCarrier": {                            "AirlineID": {                                "value": "26"                            },                            "Name": "Verteil",                            "FlightNumber": {                                "value": "105"                            }                        }                    }                \]            }        \],        "Offers": {            "Offer": \[                {                    "OfferID": {                        "value": "SP1P-5393631375348164968-1",                        "Owner": "26"                    },                    "OfferItemIDs": {                        "OfferItemID": \[                            {                                "value": "SP1P-5393631375348164968-1-29",                                "refs": \[                                    "PAX2"                                \],                                "Quantity": 1                            }                        \]                    }                }            \]        }    },    "DataLists": {        "AnonymousTravelerList": {            "AnonymousTraveler": \[                {                    "ObjectKey": "PAX2",                    "PTC": {                        "value": "ADT"                    },                    "Age": {                    },                    "Name": {                        "Surname": {                            "value": "DOE"                        },                        "Given": \[                            {                                "value": "JON"                            }                        \],                        "Title": "MR"                    }                }            \]        }    },    "ShoppingResponseID": {        "Owner": "26",        "ResponseID": {            "value": "XnyGrprDuynST7909vgA8tPF7RtbzskD-AP8ala-gME"        }    }} Once the user receives a successful FlightPriceRS, the VDC OrderChangeRQ API is constructed by adding the required fields such as OfferRefId, OfferItemRefId and pax details. A sample OrderChangeRQ would look like below,
