**Mapping Document:** 

| FlightPriceRS | OrderChangeRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferID/ObjectKey | OrderChangeRQ/Query/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/ObjectKey |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/ServiceList/Service/ServiceID/Owner  | OrderChangeRQ/Query/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/Owner |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferPrice/OfferItemID | OrderChangeRQ/Query/Order/OrderItems/OrderItem/OrderItemID/value |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferID/Owner | OrderChangeRQ/Query/Order/Details/ShoppingResponse/Owner |
| FlightPriceRS/ShoppingResponseID/ResponseID/value | OrderChangeRQ/Query/Order/Details/ShoppingResponse/ResponseID/value |
| FlightPriceRS/PricedFlightOffers/PricedFlightOffer/OfferID/Owner | OrderChangeRQ/Query/Order/OrderID/Owner |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/ServiceList/Service/Name/value | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/ServiceList/Service/Descriptions/Description/Text/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/SegmentKey | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/SegmentKey |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/AirportCode/value | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/AirportCode/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/Date | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/Date |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/Time | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/Departure/Time |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/AirlineID/value | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/AirlineID/value |
| FlightPriceRS/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/FlightNumber/value | OrderChangeRQ/DataLists/FlightSegmentList/FlightSegment/MarketingCarrier/FlightNumber/value |

#### Success {#success-21}

A successful transaction means the traveler completes addition of the selected service to the booking:

✅ **AirShopping** retrieves all relevant flights, including personalized offers.Read more details in the Concept Section.  
✅ **FlightPrice** returns the correct fare, ensuring transparency in pricing.  
✅ **OrderCreate** successfully books the flight, providing an immediate confirmation with an e-ticket (if payment is done during OrderCreate).  
✅ **OrderRetrieve** successfully retrieves the order, with relevant information like itinerary, ticket number, PNR.  
✅ **ServiceList** successfully add paid or free services to the order.  
✅ **OrderChange** successfully adds the services, providing an immediate confirmation with an EMD-ticket if the payment is successful.

[Click here to view worked example](https://drive.google.com/open?id=1iNjnhZcGbO3w8k1jHD3b_0NcvQuwxSC0)

###### 

###### 

###### 

# 

# Concepts {#concepts}

### **Offer** {#offer}

* An Offer is defined as a proposal to sell a specific set of products or Services under specific conditions, for a certain price.

### **OfferID** {#offerid}

* OfferID is a unique value given to identify an offer.  
* OfferIDs can be found in Offer APIs like AirShopping, FlightPrice, ItinReshop, Reshop.Reprice, Reshop, Reshop.Requote etc.

### **OfferItem** {#offeritem}

* The OfferItem is the sellable unit, grouping one or more services like flight/ ancillary/seat  with an associated total price for all included Services for all passengers and all segments referenced.  
* OfferItemIDs can be found in Offer APIs like AirShopping, FlightPrice, ItinReshop, Reshop.Reprice, Reshop, Reshop.Requote etc.

### **ServiceID**  {#serviceid}

* The Services are the units to be delivered by airline as part of the purchased offer or order items. In an order, a serviceID should be defined for a single passenger on a single segment.  
* The ServiceIDs will be present in the OrderViewRS   
* The ServiceID will be used in Reshop APIs to communicate the services which the customer wishes to retain as part of the change process

### **ShoppingResponseID** {#shoppingresponseid}

* The ShoppingResponseID node holds the ResponseID, which is a unique identifier for the response. This links the shopping response to the offer, enabling easy reference.

### **Services in Seat and ancillary**  {#services-in-seat-and-ancillary}

* The Services node contains details of the seat/ancillary item added. These services are defined by a ServiceID, and each service is associated with specific Traveler and Flight references.   
* The services can be linked to travelers using TravelerReferences, and to specific flight segments via SegmentReferences.  
* Services will be found in SeatAvailaibility and ServiceList

### **FlightSegmentList** {#flightsegmentlist}

* The FlightSegmentList node provides detailed information about each flight segment, such as departure and arrival details.   
* Each flight segment is identified by a SegmentKey, which is linked to a specific offer. 

### **FlightList** {#flightlist}

* The FlightList node references flights through FlightKey and SegmentReferences.   
* It provides a direct link between a flight and its corresponding flight segments, allowing for proper association between services, passengers, and segments.

### **OriginDestinationList** {#origindestinationlist}

* The OriginDestinationList node connects the departure and arrival details to specific flights.   
* The OriginDestinationKey links to a specific FlightKey in the FlightList, establishing the relationship between the origin, destination, and associated flight.  
* In case of connection flights, OriginDestinationList specifies the OD rather than the connections involved in the booking.

### **Traveler** {#traveler}

* The Traveler node provides details about the passengers, including identifiers such as TravelerReferences and TravelerID.   
* This node is for associating services, such as baggage or meals, with the correct passenger.

### **Price** {#price}

* The Price node within each OfferItem provides the total cost of an OfferItem, including any applicable charges. It includes Total, Base Fare, fees, discount, surcharge and any Taxes associated with the OfferItem, and specifies the Currency Code used for the pricing.

### **PricedInd** {#pricedind}

* The PricedInd node in seats/services indicates whether a particular service has been priced.   
* If PricedInd is true, the seat/service has a cost associated with it, which is reflected in the Price node.   
* If PricedInd is false or not provided, the chosen seats/services need to be priced using VDC FlightPrice API. Detailed in add seat and ancillary to the booking with and without pricing.

### 

### **Time Limits** {#time-limits}

OfferExpirationTimeLimit

* Selection of offers are permitted within the OfferExpirationTimeLimit. Upon expiry, the expectation is that the offer is no longer retrievable and the shopping process should begin again. 

PriceGuaranteeTimeLimit

* The duration of time the price of an offer item is guaranteed. In the absence of the price guarantee time limit, the price is not guaranteed. Upon expiry, the price may no longer be guaranteed and either a new shopping process should begin, or the offer should be repriced resulting in a new Offer.  
* Where a price guarantee time limit is applied, there is no guarantee that related inventory is guaranteed by the airline against a given offer item.

Payment Timelimit

* This field is used to indicate the duration of time by when the payment should be completed for a booking kept on hold.  
* If the payment is not completed within the payment timelimit, the booking will be cancelled.   
* It is the responsibility of the agency to cancel the Order, where the payment time limit is about to expire  and order cannot be ticketed.This is to avoid any penalties from airlines.

### **Order** {#order}

* An Order is a list of products/services that Customer(s) intend to buy or bought. An Order is composed of two main element structures: one or more OrderItem elements and, within these, one or more Service elements.

### **OrderItem** {#orderitem}

* An OrderItem has an associated OrderItemID. Each OrderItem is created from a corresponding OfferItem. As such an OrderItem is also a representation of the set of Service(s) collectively evaluated for pricing.

### **Surcharge** {#surcharge}

* Payment surcharge (Credit card surcharge/BSP fee) \- This is a fee collected by airlines for processing payments through specific credit cards. This fee is usually applied to cover the merchant service fees that airlines must pay to the credit card company.  
* Airline surcharge- Airline sometimes provides Surcharges on top of the Fee. These surcharges include items like Fuel, Carbon Offset etc


A detailed explanation on how the payment fees will be reflected in the response and how the fee can be added in the subsequent requests will be available [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1_C7RxrBWr8UgQJ8iTJD6qA9f3AzRSKaJD1hgucDRNnQ/edit?tab=t.0#heading=h.5qs67zoxb3d4)

### **GST** {#gst}

During bookings, GST (Goods and Services Tax) details such as the employer name, GSTIN, and registered address can be sent to the airline. Currently, this information can be provided only during Prime Booking. 

A detailed explanation on how the GST information can be added in the NDC API is available [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1Th2HmpwWcLGVd8l_io7Djg9DqiZAPANtFI11iTMmZ5o/edit?addon_store&tab=t.0#heading=h.kjx0p7x48jft). 

### **Fare rules** {#fare-rules}

Fare rules provide detailed information about penalties and restrictions applicable on bookings for various scenarios such as cancellation, changes, no-shows, etc. Airlines provide this information through various NDC APIs including:

* AirShoppingRS  
* FlightPriceRS  
* OrderViewRS  
* OrderReshopRS

Please read more about the detailed fare rules explanation [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1Z3hQVbF-cqHODr1knZhF8V3vS9ZP8pcmdpB3vnyJM1E/edit?usp=sharing)

**Samples:**

Following are the samples of Cancellation and Change penalties in V1 and V3:

**Sample for Cancel in PrimeBooking:**

1. **Cancel: RefundAllowed is True and CancelFee is True**

| {    "ObjectKey": "26-Cancel-2-15280-15280",    "Details": {        "Detail": \[            {                "Type": "Cancel",                "Application": {                    "Code": "2" //Prior to Departure                },                "Amounts": {                    "Amount": \[                        {                            "CurrencyAmountValue": {                                "value": 15280,                                "Code": "INR"                            },                            "AmountApplication": "MIN",                            "ApplicableFeeRemarks": {                                "Remark": \[                                    {                                        "value": "Allowed"                                    }                                \]                            }                        },                        {                            "CurrencyAmountValue": {                                "value": 15280,                                "Code": "INR"                            },                            "AmountApplication": "MAX",                            "ApplicableFeeRemarks": {                                "Remark": \[                                    {                                        "value": "Allowed"                                    }                                \]                            }                        }                    \]                }            }        \]    },    "CancelFeeInd": true, //CancelFee is applicable    "RefundableInd": true //Refund is allowed}  |

### 

### **Baggage allowance** {#baggage-allowance}

1. Each offer provides \<BaggageAllowanceRefID\> under \<BaggageAssociations\> (if available)  nodes for CarryOn and Checked-In  
2. The node \<BaggageAllowanceRefID\> will point towards BaggageAllowanceList under DataLists which will provide the piece and weight details (if available) of the baggage from the airline.  
3. Additionally Dimension details (if available) will be displayed

**CarryOnAllowanceList:**

This list contains one or more than  Carry on/hand baggage allowance details associated with offers.

There can be three possibilities of CarryonBaggage:

* **Carryon Baggage \- Weight Allowance**  
   This allowance specifies the weight limit for the carry-on baggage that a passenger is permitted to carry. The baggage must not exceed this specified weight.

* **Carryon Baggage \- Piece Allowance**  
   This allowance defines the number of carry-on items (pieces) that a passenger can bring on board. The number of pieces is limited to the specified allowance, regardless of their weight.

* **Carryon Baggage \- Piece Weight Allowance**  
   This allowance specifies both the number of pieces and the total weight allowed for those pieces. A passenger is permitted to carry a specified number of pieces, with each piece having its own weight limit.  
    
  **Scenarios Where Baggage is Not Included in the Fare**

            In the following scenarios, carry-on baggage is considered **not included in the fare**:

1. **Carryon Baggage with Piece Allowance as Zero**  
    If the carry-on baggage has a piece allowance set to **zero** (i.e., the passenger is not allowed any carry-on items), then the baggage is not included in the fare.

2. **Carryon Baggage with Piece Weight Allowance Where Piece Allowance is Zero**  
    If the carry-on baggage specifies a piece weight allowance, but the piece allowance is **zero**, then the baggage is not included in the fare.

3. **Carryon Baggage with Piece Weight and Dimension Allowance Where Piece Allowance is Zero**  
    If the baggage allowance includes both piece weight and dimension restrictions, but the piece allowance is **zero**, meaning no carry-on items are allowed, then baggage is **not included in the fare**.

**CheckedBagAllowanceList:**

This list contains one or more than one Check-in baggage allowance details associated with offers.

There can be three possibilities of CheckedBagAllowance:

* **Checked Baggage \- Weight Allowance**  
  This allowance specifies the weight limit for checked baggage that a passenger is permitted to check in. The total weight of the baggage must not exceed this specified weight.

* **Checked Baggage \- Piece Allowance**  
  This allowance defines the number of checked baggage pieces that a passenger is permitted to check in. The number of pieces is limited to the specified allowance, regardless of their weight.

* **Checked Baggage \- Piece Weight Allowance**  
  This allowance specifies both the number of pieces and the total weight allowed for those pieces. A passenger is permitted to check in a specified number of pieces, each with its own weight limit.

  **Scenarios Where Baggage is Not Included in the Fare**

* **Checked Baggage with Piece Allowance as Zero**  
  If the checked baggage has a **piece allowance set to zero** (i.e., the passenger is not allowed to check in any pieces of baggage), then the baggage is not included in the fare.

* **Checked Baggage with Piece Weight Allowance Where Piece Allowance is Zero**  
  If the checked baggage specifies a piece weight allowance, but the piece allowance is **zero**, then the baggage is not included in the fare.

* **Checked Baggage with Piece Weight and Dimension Allowance Where Piece Allowance is Zero**  
  If the baggage allowance includes both piece weight and dimension restrictions, but the piece allowance is **zero** (i.e., no pieces are allowed), then the baggage is **not included in the fare**.

**Sample:**

Following are the samples for Carry-On and Checked Baggage Allowance in Prime and Post Booking:

**PrimeBooking:**

1. **Carry-on Baggage Piece Allowance:**  
   

| {    "CarryOnAllowanceList": {        "CarryOnAllowance": \[            {                "ListKey": "26-HandBaggage-1",                "AllowanceDescription": {                    "ApplicableParty": "Traveler"                },                "PieceAllowance": \[                    {                        "ApplicableParty": "Traveler",                        "TotalQuantity": 1, //maximum piece is allowed                        "ApplicableBag": "Cabin Bag"                    }                \]            }        \]    }} |
| :---- |

### **Types of Reshop for Reissue** {#types-of-reshop-for-reissue}

When a user initiates a reissue using **OrderReshop (Reshop.V3) API**, the structure and content of the response may vary depending on the airline's implementation. Broadly, there are two types of Reshop responses a user may encounter:

**1\. Flight-Specific Reshop**

In this type, when the user initiates a reissue (e.g., a date change or cabin upgrade), the airline provides only the **new fare details** in the response.

**Key Characteristics:**

* Only the **new fare** is returned.

* No visibility into the old fare or the fare difference.

* Typically used to select flight combinations.

The price and fare details of the new fares will be available in the OrderReshop.Reshop.V3 API in the path:   
*OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/addOfferItems/reshopPriceAndFareDetails/new*

| {	"response": {		"shoppingResponseId": "XF93DAF34-33EE-4ADD-A3EE",		"reshopOffers": \[			{				"offerId": "XF93DAF34-33EE-4ADD-A3EE-1",				"ownerCode": "26",				"addOfferItems": \[					{						"offerItemId": "XF93DAF34-33EE-4ADD-A3EE-1-1",						"reshopPriceAndFareDetails": {							"new": { *//The details regarding new offer will only be present*								"price": {...}								},								"fareDetails": \[...\]							}						},						"offeredServices": \[...\]					}				\],				"offerExpirationDateTime": "2025-04-01T12:48:00Z",				"totalPrice": {...},				"journeyOverviews": \[...\],				"baggageAllowanceSpecs": \[...\],				"augRefIds": \[...\]			}		\],		"flightOffersGroup": \[...\],		"dataMap": {...},		"augmentations": {...}	}} |
| :---- |

**2\. True Reshop**

In this type, when the user initiates a reissue (e.g., a date change or cabin upgrade),the airline provides a comprehensive breakdown including the **old fare** from the original order, the **new fare** offered, the **difference** between the two, including any penalties.

 **Key Characteristics:**

* Full transparency into fare changes.

* Enables users to review and compare before confirming the reissue.

The price and fare details of new, old and diff fare are available in the path:  
*OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/addOfferItems/reshopPriceAndFareDetails/differential* under below nodes:

* **‘new’**: The price and fare details of the new offer.  
* **‘old’:** The price and fare details of the existing Order.  
* **‘diff’**: The difference between the existing order and the new reshop offer including penalty.

| {	"response": {		"shoppingResponseId": "RA1A68421-F4A-4671-B38C",		"reshopOffers": \[			{				"offerId": "RA1A68421-F4A-4671-B38C-1",				"ownerCode": "26",				"addOfferItems": \[					{						"offerItemId": "RA1A68421-F4A-4671-B38C-1-1",						"reshopPriceAndFareDetails": {							"differential": {								"differentialTypeCode": "ADD\_COL",								"diff": { *//Details regarding the difference in fare to be paid*									"price": {...},									"fareDetails": \[...\]								},								"new": { *//details regarding the new fare available for the requested change*									"price": {...},									"fareDetails": \[...\]								},								"old": { *//details regarding the old fare*									"price": {...}								},								"penaltyRefId": "3Ex\\\\\`yUtMs"							}						},						"offeredServices": \[...\]					}				\],				"offerExpirationDateTime": "2025-02-19T05:40:57Z",				"totalPrice": {...},				"journeyOverviews": \[...\],				"baggageAllowanceSpecs": \[...\],				"augRefIds": \[...\]			}		\],		"flightOffersGroup": \[...\],		"dataMap": {...},		"augmentations": {...}	}} |
| :---- |

### **Reshop Price Splitup** {#reshop-price-splitup}

When attempting a reissue using OrderReshop (Reshop.V3) and OrderReshop (Requote.V3), the Differential section provides a detailed breakdown of pricing changes involved in the modification.

This breakdown helps in understanding the financial impact of the change by comparing the Old Price, New Price, and the Difference (Diff) between the two.

**Key Components in Differential Section**

1. **DifferentialTypeCode**  
   Several types of action are possible when the customer selects a reshopped offer:  
   * **ADD\_COL:** Additional collection i.e. due amount is more than 0 the change will result into a new payment from the customer (applies to change scenarios)  
   * **ADD\_COL\_AND\_REFUND:** the change will result into both a new payment from customer and a customer refund (applies to cancellation and change scenarios)  
   * **ADD\_COL\_AND\_RESIDUAL:** the change will result into both a new payment from customer and a determined amount kept by airline for future use (applies to cancellation and change scenarios)  
   * **EVEN\_EXCHANGE:** No addition collection i.e. due amount is 0 the change does not have any financial impact to the customer (applies to change scenarios)  
   * **EVEN\_EXCHANGE:** No addition collection i.e. due amount is 0 the change does not have any financial impact to the customer (applies to change scenarios)  
   * **REFUND:** due amount is negative the change will result into a customer refund (applies to cancellation and change scenarios)  
   * **RESIDUAL:** the change will result into a determined amount kept by airline for future use (applies to cancellation and change scenarios)  
   * **REUSABLE:** the financial result of the change will be determined when customer will come back to select new journey options  
   * **REUSABLE:** the financial result of the change will be determined when customer will come back to select new journey options  
     (applies to change scenarios when customer does not yet know how to reuse his money)

2. **Old Price (old)**: Represents the total fare of the existing (original) order before any changes are made.

3. **New Price (new):** Represents the fare of the newly selected offer (after change), irrespective of the current order.

4. **Diff Price (diff)**: The difference between the Old and New price and includes the penalty (if any). The diff will have the below informations:  
   * **DuebyAirlineAmount:** Amount that the airline will return to the customer. Value is always positive.  
   * **Total amount (Duetoairline amount):** Amount that the airline will collect from the customer. Value can be positive or negative:  
     1.  Positive: Amount to be collected from customer  
     2. Negative: Amount to be refunded (in this case dueByAirlineAmount will be present)  
   * **Base amount:** Shows the difference in base fare. Can be positive, negative, or 0\.  
     Note: This field may not appear if the airline does not provide base amount value.  
   * **Taxsummaries**: This will provide the tax breakup, with information on the refundable taxes, collectable taxes, forfeited taxes etc (if applicable).  
     Note: This field may not appear if the airline does not provide tax information*.*

| {	"response": {		"shoppingResponseId": "26724H6N8EWA4",		"reshopOffers": \[			{				"offerId": "R643603CB-B905-4127-8656-1",				"ownerCode": "26",				"addOfferItems": ### **Forms of Payment** {#forms-of-payment}

#### Pay using BSP Cash {#pay-using-bsp-cash}

BSP Cash (Billing and Settlement Plan Cash) is a payment method within IATA's BSP system, which allows accredited travel agencies to remit payments to airlines using cash transactions. Samples for various use cases would look like below,

##### Prime booking  {#prime-booking}

| "Payments": {			"Payment": \[				{					"Amount": {						"value": 47302,						"Code": "INR"					},					"Method": {						"Cash": {							"CashInd": "true"						}					}				}			\]		} |
| :---- |

#### Pay using BSP Cash Card \- Pax as Payer {#pay-using-bsp-cash-card---pax-as-payer}

BSP Card (Billing and Settlement Plan Card) is a payment method within IATA's BSP system, allowing accredited travel agencies to process payments to airlines using a passenger’s prepaid card. This centralized settlement mechanism simplifies financial transactions between travel agents, passengers, and airlines

##### Prime booking  {#prime-booking-1}

#### Pay using BSP Cash Card \- Third Party credit card {#pay-using-bsp-cash-card---third-party-credit-card}

IATA's BSP system allows accredited travel agencies to process payments to airlines using a prepaid card. While typically paid by the passenger, the payment can also be made using a card from a third party, simplifying financial transactions between travel agents, passengers, third parties, and airlines. In this form of payment the third party details have to be sent in the request.

#### Pay using IATA EasyPay {#pay-using-iata-easypay}

IATA EasyPay is a voluntary payment system within the BSP, allowing travel agents to issue tickets and pay on a “pay-as-you-go” basis. This payment method requires the agent to provide the EasyPay account number and the expiration date of the account.

### **MOTO Indicator** {#moto-indicator}

* In the context of credit card (CC) payments for airline bookings, a MOTO (Mail Order/Telephone Order) transaction is a type of payment where the cardholder's credit card details are provided remotely usually over the phone or through mail without the physical presence of the card.  
* 3D Secure Exemption: MOTO transactions are often exempt from 3D Secure (3DS) authentication, which is a security protocol used to verify a cardholder’s identity in online transactions. Since MOTO transactions are initiated without the cardholder physically present, the 3DS step is typically bypassed.

##### Prime Booking {#prime-booking-4}

### **Ticket Documents** {#ticket-documents}

The ticket document will be provided for the OrderItems associated with BSP-supported carriers, while it will not be applicable for ticketless carriers. 

* When a paid booking is successfully completed, a ticket document is generated for the flight in the OrderView RS with the TypeCode “T”  
* When a paid seat is successfully added to a booking, a ticket document is generated in the OrderViewRS with TypeCode “J” (This applies to both prime and post booking)  
* When a paid ancillary is successfully added to the booking, a ticket document is generated in the OrderViewRS with TypeCode “J” (EMD-A) (This applies to both prime and post booking). The reason for issuance can be found in the ancillary’s TicketDocument node, which is linked to the OrderItem through the ServiceReferences value as the ServiceID   
* When a ticket is reissued with a penalty, a new ticket document will be generated in the OrderViewRS with the TypeCode “Y”.. The reason for issuance can be found in the new TicketDocument node, which is linked to the OrderItem through the ServiceReferences value as the ServiceID

A detailed explanation on the ticket document is available [here](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1VDk_w54ZRINDr5V5sxcMPhBRq2uQbMpsH9uhOk2c-Fo/edit?tab=t.0). 

### **Currency MetaData** {#currency-metadata}

* This node is used to determine if a decimal value calculation should be applied to the amount values  
* The user is expected to read currency metadata as follows-  
  * If the Response has this metadata, it indicates that the decimal value should be applied and modifications need to be done.  
  * if the currency metadata is absent, the actual amount can be used without any modifications.

| "CurrencyMetadatas": {						"CurrencyMetadata": \[							{								"MetadataKey": "26-INR",								"Decimals": 0							},							{								"MetadataKey": "26-GBP",								"Decimals": 2							}						\]					} |
| :---- |


* In the case of AirShopping response, you receive the currency metadata with MetadataKey in a combination format of the airline Owner and the Currency \- for example, EK-INR, WY-SAR etc. This is because, during AirShopping, offers from multiple airlines are returned, and the currency metadata is only applicable for airlines that explicitly define it.  
  Thus, only airlines providing a corresponding currency metadata must be considered for conversion or pricing logic.  
* However, when moving to the FlightPrice response, the carrier code is not visible in the currency metadata because the response is now specific to a single airline  
* From FlightPrice onwards (including subsequent services like ServiceList, SeatAvailability, OrderCreate etc.), the currency metadata is generic since the transaction pertains only to one airline and if that airline is providing the currency metadata details, same has to be considered in the logic.

### **Constructing itinerary**  {#constructing-itinerary}

#### Shopping Response {#shopping-response}

* A successful Shopping response provides multiple offers. The itinerary details have to be constructed from the details provided under the particular offer. 

* Under the **\<PricedOffer\>** you will find **\<OfferPrice\>** and **\<Associations\>.**

* The **\<OfferPrice\>** which will provide Offer price details for the travelers and **\<Associations\>** will provide details about the flights that are associated with this offer are returned.

* To start with, kindly navigate to the section called **\<AssociatedTraveler\>** inside the OfferPrice/RequestedDate/Association which will provide you the TravelerReferences

* TravelerReferences will point towards the DataLists/AnonymousTravelerList which will return the passenger details.

* Next navigate to the **\<ApplicableFlight\>** inside the OfferPrice/RequestedDate/Association which will provide FlightReferences and FlightSegmentReference.

* FlightReferences will point towards DataLists/FlightList which will return the Flight details under \<Flight\>

* FlightSegmentReference will point towards DataLists/FlightSegmentList which will return the segment details under the \<FlightSegment\>

* Next navigate to the **\<FareDetail\>** inside the OfferPrice which will provide you refs and SegmentReference.

* Refs will point towards DataLists/FareList/ListKey which will return Faretype and FareBasisCode.

* SegmentReference will point towards DataLists/FlightSegmentList which will return the segment details under the \<FlightSegment\>.

* Next navigate to the section called **\<Associations\>** inside PricedOffer which will provide you the PriceClassReference, FlightReferences and OriginDestinationReferences

* PriceClassReference will point towards DataLists/PriceClassList which will return the fare product name under the \<PriceClass\>

* FlightReferences will point towards DataLists/FlightList which will return the Flight details under \<Flight\>

* OriginDestinationReferences will point towards DataLists/OriginDestinationList which will return the Origin and Destination details under \<OriginDestination\>.

#### ReShop Response {#reshop-response}

* A successful ReShop response provides multiple offers. The itinerary details have to be constructed from the details provided under the particular offer. 

* Under the OrderReshopRS you will find \<reshopOffers\> and \<flightOffersGroup\>

* The \<reshopOffers\> will provide reshopped Offers or repriced Offer.

* The \<flightOffersGroup\> will provide Offers grouped by flight or flights combination and This is intended for displaying flight wise offers in the shopping results page. The ordering of flights will be based on sorting preference chosen by the user.

* Navigate to the \<bestOfferRefId\> inside the flightOffersGroup. This will return the Offer ID of the best offer on a flight or flights combination determined based on the sorting order chosen by the user.

* Navigate to the \<otherOfferRefIds\> inside the flightOffersGroup which will return the other offers on the same flight or flights combination.

* Next navigate to the \<paxRefIds\> inside the reshopOffers/addOfferItems/offeredServices which will point towards the dataMap/passengers which will return the passenger details.

* Next navigate to the \<serviceAssociation\> inside the reshopOffers/addOfferItems/offeredServices which will provide you paxJourneyRefs.

* paxJourneyRefs will point towards the dataMap/paxJourneys which will return the Origin and Destination details.

* Next navigate to the \<fareDetails\> which will provide you the passengerRefs and fareComponents.

* The passengerRefs  will point towards the  dataMap/passengers which will return the passenger details.

* The fareComponents will provide details about the cabinCode, cabinName, farebasis and rbd information.

* Next navigate to the \<journeyOverviews\> which will provide details about the paxJourneyRefId and priceClassRefId.

* The \<paxJourneyRefId \> will point towards the dataMap/paxJourneys which will return the Origin and Destination details.

* The \<priceClassRefId\> will point towards the dataMap/priceClasses which will return the fare product name.

### **Constructing Seat Map**  {#constructing-seat-map}

1. Identify the Offer ID for the Seat Map  
   1. Locate **SeatAvailabilityRS/Offer/ObjectKey**, which provides the unique **Offer ID**.  
   2. This ID links the seat map to a specific booking or pricing offer.

2. Extract Flight Details  
   1. Under **SeatAvailabilityRS/Flights**, find the list of flight segments for which the seat map is provided.  
   2. Identify the associated **flight segment(s)** using **FlightSegmentReferences**.  
   3. Each **flight segment** contains:  
       ✅ **Cabin layout** – The seating arrangement per deck.  
       ✅ **Exit row positions** – Rows where exit seats are located.  
       ✅ **Wing position rows** – Rows that are over the wing.

3. Understand Cabin Layout  
   1. **SeatAvailabilityRS/Flights/Cabin/CabinLayout/WingPosition** → Over-the-wing seat rows.  
   2. **SeatAvailabilityRS/Flights/Cabin/CabinLayout/ExitRowPosition** → Exit row seat positions.  
   3. **UpperDeckInd** – Indicates whether the row belongs to the upper or main deck.

4. Determine the Seat Arrangement  
   1. Seat columns are defined under **SeatDisplay/Columns**.  
   2. Columns may also have a **position indicator** (W for Window, C for Centre, A for Aisle).  
   3. Seat row positions are mapped from **First to Last** row.  
   4. Example:  
      * **Aisle seats:** Marked with A.  
      * **Window seats:** Marked with W.  
      * **Exit row seats:** Defined in ExitRowPosition.

5. Identify Special Cabin Components  
   1. Some aircraft layouts contain **non-seat components** (e.g., lavatories, galleys, emergency exits).  
   2. These components are found under **SeatDisplay/Component**, where:  
      * **Location/Row** specifies the row position.  
      * **Location/Column** specifies column position.  
      * **Type/Code** gives the **IATA-defined component type** (e.g., lavatory, wardrobe, emergency exit).

6. Check Seat Pricing and Service Details

   1. **Seat pricing is found under SeatAvailabilityRS/Services/Service**, where:  
      * Name specifies the **seat category** (e.g., "Preferred Seat", "Extra Legroom").  
      * Price/Total/value gives the **price in currency (Total/Code)**.  
      * If PricedInd \= False, a **pricing request is needed** before booking.  
      * If PricedInd \= True, **a pricing request is not needed** before booking.

7. Locate Individual Seat Details

   1. The **seat list** under **DataLists/SeatList/Seats** provides a row-by-row breakdown.  
   2. Each seat has:  
      * **Location/Column** → Seat letter (e.g., A, B, C).  
      * **Location/Row/Number/value** → Seat row number.  
      * **Characteristics/Characteristic/Remarks** → Seat availability:  
        * **"A"** → Seat is available.  
        * **"N"** → Seat is **not available**.

8. Link Seats to Their Pricing (if applicable)  
   1. If a seat has an associated price, it is referenced under **Seats/refs**.  
   2. This ref connects the seat to the corresponding **seat service pricing**.

9. Seat Characteristic code  
1. The seat characteristic codes is available in the below document

   [Verteil Codeset Directory.docx](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1v2HBoqv621MCgsL5pFL3d6sTAgBRztVS/edit?tab=t.0#heading=h.8k4zexijer34)

### **Augmentations** {#augmentations}

Augmentations are undefined elements which allow adding any structure within the message for any defined purpose.  

* They embed opaque data required for subsequent pricing, conversion of the offer into order and required for internal processing for purposes like normalizing the request.  
* These arbitrary data will be relating to offers that the API consumers are not expected to process.  
* API consumers must pass all Augmentations referred to in the offer they select in subsequent pricing calls.


**Points to Note:**

* Augmentation is **conditional.** That is, when the details are available in responses , then only they have to be supplied in next subsequent requests  
* Augmentations are generally provided inside the **metadata** section or under the **augmentations** section of the VDC APIs.

#### **Augmentation in Prime Booking Flow** {#augmentation-in-prime-booking-flow}

##### **Instant Ticketing**  {#instant-ticketing}

**Part-1 (From AirShopping to FlightPrice)**

User should expect and pass the augmentations ((if available) as below from AirShoppingRS to FlightPriceRQ:

| AirShoppingRS | FlightPriceRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| For the selected offer in **AirShoppingRS**, identify the **refs** from : *AirShoppingRS/OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/refs/Ref* This "Ref" links to the:  *AirShoppingRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey*   Along with the augmentation data: *AirShoppingRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint *  | In FlightPriceRQ, the "Ref" from AirShoppingRS should be supplied in the path: *FlightPriceRQ/Query/Offers/Offer/refs/Ref* And for this “Ref” supply the corresponding PriceMetaData for the path:  *FlightPriceRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey*  Along with the augmentation data: *FlightPriceRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint*   |

**Part-2 (From AirShopping to FlightPrice to OrderCreate)**

To fetch **detailed fare rules,** certain augmentations must be passed through the API flow.  User should expect and pass the augmentations (if available) as below from AirShoppingRS to FlightPriceRQ and from FlightPriceRS to OrderCreateRQ:

| AirShoppingRS | FlightPriceRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| For the selected offer in **AirShoppingRS**, identify the **refs** from **FareComponent**: *AirShoppingRS/OffersGroup/AirlineOffers/AirlineOffer/PricedOffer/OfferPrice/FareDetail/FareComponent/refs* This will lead to:*AirShoppingRS/DataLists/FareList/FareGroup/ListKey* From the above respective **FareGroup** Object, locate the **refs:***AirShoppingRS/DataLists/FareList/FareGroup/refs*Retrieve the corresponding Metadata:*AirShoppingRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey* Along with the augmentation data: *AirShoppingRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint*  | In the **FlightPrice RQ,** supply the corresponding **PriceMetaData** for the path *FlightPriceRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey* Along with the augmentation data:*FlightPriceRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint*   |

| FlightPriceRS | OrderCreateRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| When the augmentation data is present in **FlightPriceRS** for the path:*FlightPriceRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey*  Along with the augmentation point: *FlightPriceRS/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint*  | Supply the same set of details as it is to **OrderCreateRQ** in the path:*OrderCreateRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey*  Along with the augmentation point: *OrderCreateRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint*   |

##### **Deferred Payment** {#deferred-payment}

User should expect and pass the augmentations (if available) as below from OrderReshopRS(Reprice.v3) to OrderChangeRQ(AcceptRepricedOrder)(If available)

| OrderReshopRS(Reprice.V3) | OrderChangeRQ(AcceptRepricedOrder) |
| :---- | :---- |
| *OrderReshopRS/response/augmentations* | *OrderChangeRQ/acceptRepricedOrder/augmentations* |

#### **Augmentation in Post Booking Flow** {#augmentation-in-post-booking-flow}

##### **Cancellation** {#cancellation}

1. **Paid Booking**  
   User is expected and pass the augmentations (if available) as below from ItinReshopRS to OrderCancelRQ

| ItinReshopRS | OrderCancelRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| Identify the refs in ItinReshopRS: *ItinReshopRS/Response/Order/refs/Ref*This will refer to MetadataKey which should be retrieved: *ItinReshopRS/Response/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey* Along with the augmentation data: *ItinReshopRS/Response/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint* | *The ref from ItinReshop to be passed in:OrderCancelRQ/Query/OrderID/refs/Ref*And for this “Ref” supply the corresponding PriceMetaData for the path:  *OrderCancelRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/MetadataKey*  Along with the augmentation data: *OrderCancelRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata/PriceMetadatas/PriceMetadata/AugmentationPoint*  |

2. **Unpaid Booking**  
   User should expect and pass the augmentations (if available) as below from OrderReshopRS to OrderCancelRQ

| OrderReshopRS (Reprice.V3) | OrderCancelRQ |
| :---- | :---- |
| *OrderReshopRS/response/augmentations* | *OrderCancelRQ/Metadata/Other/OtherMetadata* |

##### **Reissue** {#reissue-5}

User should expect and pass the augmentations (if available) as below from  OrderReshopRS(Reshop.V3) to OrderReshopRQ(Requote.V3) and from OrderReshopRS(Requote.V3) to OrderChangeRQ(AcceptOrderItems.V3)

| OrderReshopRS(Reshop.V3) | OrderReshopRQ(Requote.V3) |
| :---- | :---- |
| For the selected offer, identify the **refs** inside *OrderReshopRS/response/reshopOffers/augRefIds* And this **refs** links to the following path:*OrderReshopRS/response/augmentations* | In the OrderReshopRQ(Requote.V3) this should be passed to:*OrderReshopRQ/requoteOrder/augmentations*  |

| OrderReshopRS(Requote.V3) | OrderChangeRQ(UpdateOrderItems.V3) |
| :---- | :---- |
| *OrderReshopRS/response/augmentations* | *OrderChangeRQ/updateOrderItems/augmentations* |
